# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 011 SECRET ALLIANCE EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# Event-owned runtime for fixed targeting, weighted founder/member selection,
# motives and doctrine, concealed progression, counter-network outcomes,
# reveal convergence, coalition war, settlement, scenarios, and achievements.
# ============================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PREFIRE, SELECTION, AND INITIALIZATION
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

secret_alliance_clear_prefire_context = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_prefire_candidate
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_target
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_ready = 0 }
}

secret_alliance_prepare_random_event_fire = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_ready = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_automatic_event_is_available = yes }
		save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_target
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_members
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_founders
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_sponsors
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspect_cleanup_archive
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_confirmed_members
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_turned_members
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 0 }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = 0 }
		secret_alliance_refresh_prefire_evolution_state = yes
		secret_alliance_build_weighted_minor_member_pool = yes
		secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = yes
		secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = yes
		secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = yes
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = constant:secret_alliance_event.normal_founders } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_ready = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			secret_alliance_abort_unstarted_context = yes
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_refresh_prefire_evolution_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:secret_alliance_event.id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_type }

	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_stage }
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_stage }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_stage }
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_stage }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_stage }
	if = {
		limit = { is_current_evolution_enabled = yes }
		# A pre-fire stage III opening deliberately begins with the stage II
		# package and a short real preparation window before stage III.
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_pending = 1 }
	}
}

secret_alliance_score_current_minor_candidate = {
	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_weight_candidate
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.base }
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_faction = no }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.factionless }
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.existing_faction_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.target_neighbor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.same_continent }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.ideological_rival }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { is_major = yes } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.target_major }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_controlled_states < constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.weak_country_state_threshold }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.weak_country }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_generated_tension > constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.medium_tension_threshold } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.target_tension_medium }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_generated_tension > constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.high_tension_threshold } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.target_tension_high }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.regional_ring }
			}
			is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.regional_neighbor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.regional_ring }
			}
			is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.regional_continent }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.ideological_front }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.ideological_opponent }
		}
		else = {
			multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.ideological_same_penalty }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.great_power_sponsor }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.great_partner_neighbor } }
		if = { limit = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.great_partner_continent } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes }
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.great_partner_maritime }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					gives_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.great_partner_access }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.unlikely_coalition }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.unlikely_factioned } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.unlikely_distant } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.unlikely_non_neighbor } }
	}
	secret_alliance_apply_minor_candidate_capacity_weights = yes
	round_temp_variable = secret_alliance_candidate_weight
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_candidate_weight
		min = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.minimum_ticket
		max = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.maximum_ticket
	}
}

secret_alliance_add_current_minor_candidate_to_weighted_pool = {
	secret_alliance_score_current_minor_candidate = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
		add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool = THIS }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
	}
}

secret_alliance_build_weighted_minor_member_pool = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes }
		secret_alliance_score_current_minor_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool = THIS }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_build_weighted_matching_ideology_minor_pool = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes
			has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		}
		secret_alliance_score_current_minor_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool = THIS }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_build_weighted_opposing_ideology_minor_pool = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes
			NOT = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		}
		secret_alliance_score_current_minor_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool = THIS }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
			secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool value = THIS } }
				remove_from_temp_array = { array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool value = THIS }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_select_weighted_minor_member = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes }
		secret_alliance_add_current_minor_candidate_to_weighted_pool = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool^num > 0 } }
		 random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
			secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
}

secret_alliance_score_current_major_candidate = {
	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_weight_candidate
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.base }
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_faction = no }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.factionless }
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.existing_faction_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.same_continent }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.ideological_rival }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_generated_tension > constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.medium_tension_threshold } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.target_tension_high }
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_ai.major_sponsor_bonus }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.great_power_sponsor }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_weight.great_sponsor_major }
	}
	secret_alliance_apply_major_candidate_capacity_weights = yes
	round_temp_variable = secret_alliance_candidate_weight
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_candidate_weight
		min = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.minimum_ticket
		max = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.maximum_ticket
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_minor_candidate_capacity_weights = {
	if = { limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { any_owned_state = { OR = { is_claimed_by = event_target:secret_alliance_weight_candidate is_core_of = event_target:secret_alliance_weight_candidate } } } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.claim_or_core_grievance } }
	if = { limit = { has_opinion = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_target value < 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.negative_opinion_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_opinion = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_target value > 50 } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.positive_opinion_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target gives_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.access_dependence_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target } NOT = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.distant_interest_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { casualties > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.recent_losses_casualties } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.recent_losses_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.active_war_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { has_stability < constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { has_stability > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.high_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.high_stability_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.minor_factory_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.industrial_capacity_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.minor_equipment_threshold } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.equipment_capacity_bonus } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major }
		if = { limit = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_first_major } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_ideology_support } }
		if = { limit = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_first_major } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_continent_support } }
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_major_candidate_capacity_weights = {
	if = { limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { any_owned_state = { OR = { is_claimed_by = event_target:secret_alliance_weight_candidate is_core_of = event_target:secret_alliance_weight_candidate } } } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.claim_or_core_grievance } }
	if = { limit = { has_opinion = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_target value < 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.negative_opinion_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_opinion = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_target value > 50 } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.positive_opinion_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { has_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target gives_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.access_dependence_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { is_on_same_continent_as = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.distant_interest_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { casualties > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.recent_losses_casualties } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.recent_losses_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.active_war_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { has_stability < constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_penalty } }
	if = { limit = { has_stability > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.high_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.high_stability_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.major_factory_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.industrial_capacity_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.major_equipment_threshold } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.equipment_capacity_bonus } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major }
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = constant:secret_alliance_sponsor_theater.land_front } }
				NOT = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
				any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_theater_complement_bonus }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = constant:secret_alliance_sponsor_theater.maritime } }
				is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_theater_complement_bonus }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = constant:secret_alliance_sponsor_theater.distant_support } }
				OR = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_theater_complement_bonus }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_select_weighted_major_member = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_major_sponsor = yes }
		secret_alliance_score_current_major_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool = THIS }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
			secret_alliance_register_major_member = yes
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
}

secret_alliance_build_weighted_major_member_pool = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_major_sponsor = yes }
		secret_alliance_score_current_major_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool = THIS }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_draw_weighted_major_member_from_pool = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool
			secret_alliance_register_major_member = yes
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool value = THIS } }
				remove_from_temp_array = { array = secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool value = THIS }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_select_minor_invitation_candidate = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_human_invitation_pool
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_candidate_selected = 0 }
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_human_recruit = yes }
		add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_human_invitation_pool = THIS }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_human_invitation_pool^num > 0 } }
		random = {
			chance = constant:secret_alliance_operation.human_invitation_chance
			random_scope_in_array = {
				array = secret_alliance_human_invitation_pool
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
			}
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_candidate_selected = 1 }
		}
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes }
		secret_alliance_add_current_minor_candidate_to_weighted_pool = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_candidate_selected < 1 }
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool^num > 0 }
		}
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
			save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_human_invitation_pool
}

secret_alliance_select_major_sponsor_candidate = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_major_sponsor = yes }
		secret_alliance_score_current_major_candidate = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = secret_alliance_candidate_weight }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool = THIS }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_tickets_remaining = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
			save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_candidate_pool
}

secret_alliance_schedule_minor_invitation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending }
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_select_minor_invitation_candidate = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_next_invitation_days = mtth:secret_alliance_invitation_interval }
				clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_next_invitation_days min = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_interval_min max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_interval_max }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = { is_ai = yes } }
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.18 days = secret_alliance_next_invitation_days } }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.20 days = secret_alliance_next_invitation_days } }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_human_invitation_join = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_human_recruit = yes }
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
		secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.accepted }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_accepted_history = 1 }
	}
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
}

secret_alliance_human_invitation_conditional = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_human_recruit = yes }
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
		secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_conditional_recruit
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_operation.conditional_accept_commitment }
		secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.conditional }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_conditional_history = 1 }
	}
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
}

secret_alliance_human_invitation_refuse = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.refused }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_refused_history = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_cohesion }
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
}

secret_alliance_human_invitation_leak = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
	secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.leaked }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_leaked_history = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_leak_pause_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.major_leak_pause_days }
	set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recruitment_paused_by_leak days = secret_alliance_leak_pause_days }
	set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recent_leak days = secret_alliance_leak_pause_days }
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
}

secret_alliance_human_invitation_expose = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.captured_conference }
	secret_alliance_reveal_pact = yes
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
}

secret_alliance_schedule_sponsor_approach = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending }
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_select_major_sponsor_candidate = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_next_sponsor_days = mtth:secret_alliance_sponsor_interval }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = secret_alliance_next_sponsor_days
					min = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_interval_min
					max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_interval_max
				}
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.19 days = secret_alliance_next_sponsor_days }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_resolve_minor_invitation = {
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.none }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
			event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = { secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes }
		}
		secret_alliance_prepare_minor_invitation_response_weights = yes
		random_list = {
			secret_alliance_invitation_accept_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.accepted }
				event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
					secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.recruitment_cohesion }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.recruitment_readiness }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_accepted_history = 1 }
			}
			secret_alliance_invitation_conditional_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.conditional }
				event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
					secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_conditional_recruit
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_operation.conditional_accept_commitment }
					secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_conditional_history = 1 }
			}
			secret_alliance_invitation_refuse_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.refused }
				event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_cohesion }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_refused_history = 1 }
			}
			secret_alliance_invitation_leak_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.leaked }
				event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_invitation_pending
					secret_alliance_register_current_country_as_suspect = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
				event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = { save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source }
				secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_invitation_leaked_history = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_leak_pause_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.major_leak_pause_days }
				set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recruitment_paused_by_leak days = secret_alliance_leak_pause_days }
				set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recent_leak days = secret_alliance_leak_pause_days }
			}
		}
	}
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_invitation_candidate
}

secret_alliance_resolve_sponsor_approach = {
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_attempt_pulse = global.secret_alliance_operation_pulses }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.none }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate
			event_target:secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate = { secret_alliance_is_valid_major_sponsor = yes }
		}
		secret_alliance_prepare_sponsor_response_weights = yes
		random_list = {
			secret_alliance_sponsor_accept_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.accepted }
				event_target:secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending
					secret_alliance_register_major_member = yes
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.sponsor_cohesion }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.sponsor_readiness }
			}
			secret_alliance_sponsor_refuse_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.refused }
				event_target:secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending
				}
				set_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_refusal_dispute
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_cohesion }
			}
			secret_alliance_sponsor_leak_weight = {
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_recruitment_outcome.leaked }
				event_target:secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate = {
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending
					secret_alliance_register_current_country_as_suspect = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
				event_target:secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate = { save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source }
				secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
			}
		}
	}
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate
	secret_alliance_determine_doctrine = yes
}

secret_alliance_prepare_minor_invitation_response_weights = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.invitation_accept_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_conditional_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.invitation_conditional_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_refuse_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.invitation_refuse_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_leak_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.invitation_leak_weight }
	event_target:secret_alliance_invitation_candidate = {
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_refuse_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_refuse_war_bonus } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_leak_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_leak_war_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { has_stability < constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_refuse_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_refuse_instability_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.minor_factory_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_accept_resource_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.minor_equipment_threshold } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.equipment_capacity_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_first_major } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_invitation_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_ideology_support } }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_invitation_accept_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_invitation_conditional_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_invitation_refuse_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_invitation_leak_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
}

secret_alliance_prepare_sponsor_response_weights = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.sponsor_accept_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_refuse_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.sponsor_refuse_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_leak_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.sponsor_leak_weight }
	event_target:secret_alliance_sponsor_candidate = {
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_refuse_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_refuse_war_bonus } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_leak_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_leak_war_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { has_stability < constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_refuse_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_refuse_instability_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.major_factory_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_accept_resource_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.major_equipment_threshold } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.equipment_capacity_bonus } }
		if = { limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_generated_tension > constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.medium_tension_threshold } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_accept_weight = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.sponsor_accept_reason_bonus } }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_sponsor_accept_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_sponsor_refuse_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_sponsor_leak_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.response_weight_max }
}

secret_alliance_assign_current_member_motive = {
	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_motive_candidate
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_fear_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.fear }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_grievance_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.grievance }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_ideology_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.ideology }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_patronage_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.patronage }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_opportunism_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.opportunism }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_regime_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.regime_survival }
	if = { limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_fear_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.neighbor_fear_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_ideology_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.ideological_rival_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_opinion = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_target value < 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_grievance_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.negative_opinion_grievance_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { any_owned_state = { OR = { is_claimed_by = event_target:secret_alliance_motive_candidate is_core_of = event_target:secret_alliance_motive_candidate } } } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_grievance_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.claim_grievance_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major NOT = { is_major = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_patronage_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.sponsor_patronage_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_target_aggression } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_fear_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.target_aggression_fear_bonus } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_grievance_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.target_aggression_grievance_bonus } }
	if = { limit = { has_stability < constant:secret_alliance_candidate_behavior.low_stability_threshold } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_motive_regime_weight = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.unstable_regime_survival_bonus } }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_motive_fear_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_motive_grievance_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_motive_ideology_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_motive_patronage_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_motive_opportunism_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_motive_regime_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_motive_weight.maximum }
	random_list = {
		secret_alliance_motive_fear_weight = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.fear } }
		secret_alliance_motive_grievance_weight = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance } }
		secret_alliance_motive_ideology_weight = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.ideology } }
		secret_alliance_motive_patronage_weight = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.patronage } }
		secret_alliance_motive_opportunism_weight = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism } }
		secret_alliance_motive_regime_weight = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.regime_survival } }
	}
}

secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_commitment_score
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.tentative }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score > constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_tentative_max } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.conditional }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score > constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_conditional_max } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score > constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_firm_max } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.irrevocable }
	}
}

secret_alliance_add_current_operation_actor_tickets = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.operation_actor_tentative_tickets }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.conditional } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.operation_actor_conditional_tickets }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.operation_actor_firm_tickets }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.irrevocable } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.operation_actor_irrevocable_tickets }
	}
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets > constant:secret_alliance_state.false } }
		add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool = THIS }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_state.true }
	}
}

secret_alliance_add_current_defection_tickets = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.defection_tentative_tickets }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.conditional } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.defection_conditional_tickets }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.defection_firm_tickets }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.irrevocable } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.defection_irrevocable_tickets }
	}
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets > constant:secret_alliance_state.false } }
		add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_defection_pool = THIS }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_tickets = constant:secret_alliance_state.true }
	}
}

secret_alliance_assign_current_member_profile = {
	# Durable member facts: commitment, operational role, support capability,
	# and the private promise whose failure can later fracture the coalition.
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_base }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_founder }
		add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_founder_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_major_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_neighbor_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
		add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.commitment_factioned_penalty }
	}
	secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes

	if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.sponsor }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.transport_access }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		random_list = {
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.border_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.land_access }
			}
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.intelligence_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.intelligence }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.cipher_access }
			}
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.liaison_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.liaison }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.diplomatic_access }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.logistics_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics }
				if = {
					limit = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.maritime_access }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.transport_access } }
			}
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.intelligence_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.intelligence }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.cipher_access }
			}
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.political_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.political_access }
			}
			constant:secret_alliance_member_profile.liaison_weight = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.liaison }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.diplomatic_access }
			}
		}
	}

	set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.security_restraint }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.claim_settlement }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.ideology } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.political_change }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.patronage } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.sponsor_protection }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.postwar_gain }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.regime_survival } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.noninterference }
	}
}

secret_alliance_register_minor_member = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_capital_loss_recorded
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_left_prior_faction_for_reveal
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.normal_founders } }
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_founder
		add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_founders = THIS }
	}
	add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_members = THIS }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive value = THIS } } }
		add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive = THIS }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 1 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence = 0 }
	secret_alliance_assign_current_member_motive = yes
	secret_alliance_assign_current_member_profile = yes
	add_ai_strategy = {
		type = antagonize
		id = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.secret_antagonize
	}
	add_ai_strategy = {
		type = prepare_for_war
		id = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.secret_prepare_for_war
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_founder } }
		save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_first_founder
	}
}

secret_alliance_register_major_member = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_capital_loss_recorded
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_left_prior_faction_for_reveal
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_at_entry
	add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_members = THIS }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive value = THIS } } }
		add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive = THIS }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = 1 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence = 0 }
	secret_alliance_assign_current_member_motive = yes
	secret_alliance_assign_current_member_profile = yes
	secret_alliance_assign_current_sponsor_strategy = yes
	add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_sponsors = THIS }
	add_ai_strategy = {
		type = antagonize
		id = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.secret_antagonize
	}
	add_ai_strategy = {
		type = prepare_for_war
		id = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.secret_prepare_for_war
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major } }
		save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_first_major
	}
}

secret_alliance_assign_current_sponsor_strategy = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = constant:secret_alliance_sponsor_theater.distant_support }
	if = {
		limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = constant:secret_alliance_sponsor_theater.land_front }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = constant:secret_alliance_sponsor_theater.maritime }
	}
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_doctrine_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_doctrine_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.punitive }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.ideology }
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.regime_survival }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_doctrine_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.regime_pressure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.patronage }
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_doctrine_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.spoils }
	}
}

secret_alliance_determine_doctrine = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_fear_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_grievance_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ideology_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_opportunism_count = 0 }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.fear } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_fear_count = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_grievance_count = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.ideology } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ideology_count = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_opportunism_count = 1 }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_doctrine_best = secret_alliance_fear_count }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_grievance_count > secret_alliance_doctrine_best } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.punitive }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_doctrine_best = secret_alliance_grievance_count }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_ideology_count > secret_alliance_doctrine_best } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.regime_pressure }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_doctrine_best = secret_alliance_ideology_count }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_opportunism_count > secret_alliance_doctrine_best } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.spoils }
	}
}

secret_alliance_initialize_faction_name_grammar = {
	# Target scope. Country packages can set the persistent exception flag for a
	# missing or awkward adjective without modifying Event 011.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_dynamic_country = yes
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_faction_name_country_exception
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_use_country_faction_name
	}
	else = { clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_use_country_faction_name }
}

secret_alliance_initialize_event_context = {
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_normal_origin
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evolution_stage = secret_alliance_prefire_evolution }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_pulses = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_route = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_member_count = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_major_count = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_independent_evidence_classes = 0 }
	clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_fracture_reserve
	secret_alliance_determine_doctrine = yes

	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.normal_cohesion }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.normal_readiness }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.normal_alertness }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.normal_evidence }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.normal_preparedness }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_baseline = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.normal_preparedness } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution > 0 } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_i_cohesion }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_i_readiness }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution > 1 } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_ii_cohesion }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_ii_readiness }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_ii_evidence }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_ii_preparedness }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_baseline = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_ii_preparedness } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_pending > 0 } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_iii_cohesion }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_iii_readiness }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_iii_evidence }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_iii_preparedness }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_baseline = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.evolution_iii_preparedness } }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution > 0 } }
		secret_alliance_open_evolution_i = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution > 1 } }
		secret_alliance_open_evolution_ii = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_pending > 0 } }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_pending
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_delay = constant:secret_alliance_operation.prefire_evolution_iii_delay }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.17 days = secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_delay }
		}
	}

	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		secret_alliance_reset_target_run_snapshots = yes
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_country
		secret_alliance_initialize_faction_name_grammar = yes
		clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_maximum
		clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_high_or_maximum
		set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_normal_origin
		clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_forced_origin
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_debug = yes
					has_country_flag = force_trigger_mode_enabled
					has_global_flag = secret_alliance_ai_test_origin
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_forced_origin
		}
		capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_target_starting_capital }
		secret_alliance_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
		add_ideas = secret_alliance_unexplained_interference
		secret_alliance_schedule_next_concealed_pulse = yes
	}
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_launch_in_progress
}

secret_alliance_schedule_next_concealed_pulse = {
	# Target scope. Store MTTH output first because event delay parsing is more
	# reliable with a normal variable than a direct expression token.
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_next_operation_days = mtth:secret_alliance_operation_interval }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_next_operation_days
		min = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_interval_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_interval_max
	}
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.2 days = secret_alliance_next_operation_days }
}

secret_alliance_abort_unstarted_context = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive
		secret_alliance_remove_current_member_runtime = yes
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_suspect_cleanup_archive
		secret_alliance_remove_current_suspect_runtime = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_target }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_maximum
			clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_high_or_maximum
		}
	}
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_members
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_founders
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_sponsors
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspect_cleanup_archive
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_confirmed_members
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_turned_members
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_first_founder
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_target
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = 0 }
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_normal_origin
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_response_category_open
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_force_full_call
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_allow_ai_target_testing
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_ai_test_origin
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prefire_ready = 0 }
}

secret_alliance_refresh_dynamic_costs = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_scale = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.standard_scale }
	if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_scale = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.major_target_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories > constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.large_industry_threshold }
		multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_scale = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.large_industry_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_divisions > constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.large_army_threshold }
		multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_scale = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.large_army_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_controlled_states > constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.large_territory_threshold }
		multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_scale = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.large_territory_scale }
	}
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_command_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_command_power }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_army_experience = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_army_experience }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_support_equipment = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_support_equipment }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_trains = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_trains }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_trucks = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_trucks }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_convoys = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_convoys }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_fuel = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_fuel }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_manpower = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_manpower }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.base_political_power }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_investigation_stability = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.investigation_minor_stability_loss }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_investigation_stability = -1 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_intermediary_stability = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.investigation_stability_loss }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_intermediary_stability = -1 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_diplomacy_command_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.diplomacy_command_power_gate }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_diplomacy_stability = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.diplomacy_stability_loss }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_diplomacy_stability = -1 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_emergency_stability = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.emergency_stability_loss }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_emergency_stability = -1 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_partial_dossier_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.partial_dossier_political_power_gate }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_name_member_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.name_member_political_power_gate }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_expose_sponsor_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.expose_sponsor_political_power_gate }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_coalition_case_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.coalition_case_political_power_gate }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_preemption_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.preemption_weak_evidence_cost }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_complete_network } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_preemption_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.preemption_complete_evidence_cost }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_name_member } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_preemption_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.preemption_strong_evidence_cost }
	}
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_gate_preemption_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.preemption_war_support_gate }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_border_escalation_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.border_escalation_war_support_gate }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_command_power = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_army_experience = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_support_equipment = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_trains = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_trucks = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_convoys = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_fuel = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_manpower = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_cost_political_power = secret_alliance_cost_scale }
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_command_power
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_army_experience
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_support_equipment
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_trains
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_trucks
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_convoys
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_fuel
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_manpower
	round_variable = secret_alliance_cost_political_power
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = secret_alliance_active_investigations } }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_active_investigations = 0 }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_active_protections = 0 }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_active_diplomacy = 0 }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_active_offensive = 0 }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_active_border = 0 }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_active_emergency = 0 }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# EVOLUTIONS, CONCEALED PACT PULSES, AND OPERATIONS

secret_alliance_refresh_gui_meter_positions = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_evidence_fill_x = global.secret_alliance_evidence }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_evidence_fill_x = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_scale }
	subtract_from_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_evidence_fill_x = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_width }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_gui_evidence_fill_x min = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_min max = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_max }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_preparedness_fill_x = global.secret_alliance_preparedness }
	multiply_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_preparedness_fill_x = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_scale }
	subtract_from_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_preparedness_fill_x = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_width }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_gui_preparedness_fill_x min = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_min max = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.gui_meter_max }
}

secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = {
	clamp_variable = {
		var = global.secret_alliance_cohesion
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = global.secret_alliance_readiness
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = global.secret_alliance_alertness
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = global.secret_alliance_evidence
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = global.secret_alliance_preparedness
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = global.secret_alliance_war_pressure
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_target }
