		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { secret_alliance_refresh_gui_meter_positions = yes }
	}
}

secret_alliance_record_evolution = {
	# Input: temp variables secret_alliance_evolution_stage_to_record and
	# secret_alliance_evolution_tier_to_record.
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:secret_alliance_event.id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = secret_alliance_evolution_stage_to_record }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = secret_alliance_evolution_tier_to_record }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 1 }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor }
	record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
}

secret_alliance_record_open_evolutions_if_needed = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i_logged
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_evolution_stage_to_record = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_evolution_tier_to_record = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_tier }
		secret_alliance_record_evolution = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii_logged
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_evolution_stage_to_record = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_evolution_tier_to_record = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_tier }
		secret_alliance_record_evolution = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii_logged
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_evolution_stage_to_record = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_evolution_tier_to_record = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_tier }
		secret_alliance_record_evolution = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_open_evolution_i = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i } }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_stage }
		# Evolution I opens a wider pool and accelerates one invitation. It does
		# not manufacture the typical roster minimum.
		secret_alliance_schedule_minor_invitation = yes
		secret_alliance_determine_doctrine = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_open_evolution_ii = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii } }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_stage }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_major_cap } }
			secret_alliance_schedule_sponsor_approach = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_determine_doctrine = yes
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.4 days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.serious_incident_delay_days }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_open_evolution_iii = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii } }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_pending
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		secret_alliance_maybe_schedule_second_sponsor = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.war_pressure_major }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_warning_active
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			secret_alliance_cancel_target_missions = yes
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_public_coalition_pressure
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.5 days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.warning_notice_delay_days }
			activate_mission = secret_alliance_coalition_countdown
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_maybe_schedule_second_sponsor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_major_cap }
			NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending }
		}
		secret_alliance_schedule_sponsor_approach = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_major_cap }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_pulses > constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.second_sponsor_minimum_pulses }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_approach_pending }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_second_sponsor_elapsed_pulses = global.secret_alliance_operation_pulses }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_attempt_pulse }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_second_sponsor_elapsed_pulses = global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_attempt_pulse }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_second_sponsor_elapsed_pulses > constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.second_sponsor_retry_pulses } }
			secret_alliance_schedule_sponsor_approach = yes
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_refresh_member_validity = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_surviving_members
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_surviving_founders
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_surviving_sponsors
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = 0 }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_surviving_members = THIS }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_founder }
				add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_surviving_founders = THIS }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_at_entry }
				add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_surviving_sponsors = THIS }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = 1 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
			clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.defection_cohesion }
		}
	}
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_members
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_founders
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_sponsors
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = secret_alliance_surviving_members add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_members = THIS } }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = secret_alliance_surviving_founders add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_founders = THIS } }
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = secret_alliance_surviving_sponsors add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_sponsors = THIS } }
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_surviving_members
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_surviving_founders
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_surviving_sponsors
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.minimum_active_members }
		}
		secret_alliance_collapse_hidden_pact = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion < constant:secret_alliance_band.cohesion_collapse }
		}
		secret_alliance_collapse_hidden_pact = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_evaluate_evolutions = {
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:secret_alliance_event.id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_can_open_evolution_i = yes
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_open_evolution_i = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_can_open_evolution_ii = yes
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_open_evolution_ii = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_tier }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				secret_alliance_can_open_evolution_iii = yes
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_prefire_evolution_iii_pending
			}
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_open_evolution_iii = yes
	}
	secret_alliance_record_open_evolutions_if_needed = yes
}

secret_alliance_calculate_operation_chance = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = constant:secret_alliance_operation.base_operation_chance }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = global.secret_alliance_member_count }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_value_component = global.secret_alliance_readiness }
	divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_value_component = constant:secret_alliance_operation.readiness_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = secret_alliance_operation_value_component }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_value_component = global.secret_alliance_preparedness }
	divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_value_component = constant:secret_alliance_operation.preparedness_divisor }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = secret_alliance_operation_value_component }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_value_component = global.secret_alliance_alertness }
	divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_value_component = constant:secret_alliance_operation.alertness_divisor }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = secret_alliance_operation_value_component }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = constant:secret_alliance_operation.evolution_i_operation_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = constant:secret_alliance_operation.evolution_ii_operation_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_agenda_delayed_by_dispute }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_chance = constant:secret_alliance_operation.dispute_operation_penalty }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_operation_chance
		min = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_chance_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_chance_max
	}
}

secret_alliance_run_concealed_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_pulses = 1 }
		secret_alliance_refresh_member_validity = yes
		secret_alliance_refresh_suspect_validity = yes
		secret_alliance_evaluate_evolutions = yes
		secret_alliance_maybe_schedule_second_sponsor = yes
		secret_alliance_calculate_operation_chance = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_none_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_min }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_recruitment_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_dispute_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_leak_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_defection_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_operation_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_cleanup_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_false_traffic_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_member_cover_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_accelerate_reveal_weight = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recruitment_paused_by_leak } check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_member_cap } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_recruitment_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.recruitment_base_chance } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count > constant:secret_alliance_state.true } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_dispute_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.dispute_base_chance } }
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_leak_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.leak_base_chance } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_component = global.secret_alliance_alertness } divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_component = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_alertness_divisor } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_leak_weight = secret_alliance_incident_component } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion < constant:secret_alliance_band.cohesion_uneasy } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_defection_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation.defection_base_chance } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_component = constant:secret_alliance_band.cohesion_uneasy } subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_component = global.secret_alliance_cohesion } divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_component = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_cohesion_divisor } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_defection_weight = secret_alliance_incident_component } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_operation_recovery } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_operation_weight = secret_alliance_operation_chance } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_partial_dossier } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_cleanup_weight = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.cleanup_weight } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_name_member } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_false_traffic_weight = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.false_traffic_weight } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_member_cover_weight = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.member_cover_weight } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_coalition_case } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_incident_accelerate_reveal_weight = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.accelerate_reveal_weight } }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_recruitment_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_dispute_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_leak_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_defection_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_operation_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_cleanup_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_false_traffic_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_member_cover_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_incident_accelerate_reveal_weight min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_operation.incident_weight_max }
		random_list = {
			secret_alliance_incident_none_weight = { }
			secret_alliance_incident_recruitment_weight = { secret_alliance_maybe_recruit_member = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_dispute_weight = { secret_alliance_maybe_run_internal_dispute = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_leak_weight = { secret_alliance_maybe_run_leak = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_defection_weight = { secret_alliance_maybe_run_defection = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_operation_weight = { secret_alliance_launch_weighted_operation = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_cleanup_weight = { secret_alliance_pact_cleanup_exposed_routes = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_false_traffic_weight = { secret_alliance_pact_plant_false_traffic = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_member_cover_weight = { secret_alliance_pact_protect_exposed_member = yes }
			secret_alliance_incident_accelerate_reveal_weight = { secret_alliance_pact_accelerate_reveal = yes }
		}
		secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			secret_alliance_refresh_dynamic_costs = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				secret_alliance_run_target_ai_response = yes
			}
			secret_alliance_schedule_next_concealed_pulse = yes
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_pact_cleanup_exposed_routes = {
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.cleanup_evidence }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.cleanup_alertness }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_cleanup_recovery_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.recovery_days }
	set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_operation_recovery days = secret_alliance_cleanup_recovery_days }
}

secret_alliance_pact_plant_false_traffic = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_false_traffic_days = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.false_traffic_days }
	set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_false_traffic_active days = secret_alliance_false_traffic_days }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.communications } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.method } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.financial } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.military } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.human } }
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_false_traffic_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			is_ai = yes
			uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes
			NOT = { is_special_chaos_country = yes }
			NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect_cleared }
		}
		add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_false_traffic_pool = THIS }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_false_traffic_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_false_traffic_pool
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_lead_source
			save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source
		}
		secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_false_traffic_pool
}

secret_alliance_pact_protect_exposed_member = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_exposed_member_pool
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_possible }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_exposed_member_pool = THIS }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_exposed_member_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_exposed_member_pool
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_member_cover_days = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.member_cover_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_member_under_cover days = secret_alliance_member_cover_days }
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.member_cover_confidence }
			clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }
		}
		secret_alliance_rebuild_visible_suspects = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_exposed_member_pool
}

secret_alliance_pact_accelerate_reveal = {
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.accelerate_readiness }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_response.accelerate_war_pressure }
	}
}

secret_alliance_ai_select_action_target = {
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_ai_action_target
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_best_target_score = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
					secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
					has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence }
				if = { limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.action_target_neighbor_bonus } }
				if = { limit = { is_major = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.action_target_major_bonus } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score > secret_alliance_ai_best_target_score } }
					set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_best_target_score = secret_alliance_ai_target_score }
					save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_ai_action_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
			if = {
				limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_suspect = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence }
				if = { limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.action_target_neighbor_bonus } }
				if = { limit = { is_major = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.action_target_major_bonus } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_target_score > secret_alliance_ai_best_target_score } }
					set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_ai_best_target_score = secret_alliance_ai_target_score }
					save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_ai_action_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_ai_action_target }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = event_target:secret_alliance_ai_action_target }
	}
}

secret_alliance_run_target_ai_response = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_target_ai_can_respond = yes }
		secret_alliance_ai_select_action_target = yes
		if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_can_start_offensive_action = yes
			secret_alliance_selected_suspect_is_likely = yes
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_name_member }
		}
		secret_alliance_offensive_turn_member = yes
		}
		else_if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_can_start_protection = yes
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness < constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } }
			secret_alliance_protect_secure_industry = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation }
				secret_alliance_has_dynamic_train_cost = yes
			}
			secret_alliance_protect_harden_border = yes
		}
		else = { secret_alliance_protect_compartmentalize_plans = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_can_start_investigation = yes }
		secret_alliance_pay_investigation_cost = yes
		secret_alliance_resolve_weighted_counter_network_action = yes
		}
	}
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_ai_action_target
}

secret_alliance_refresh_suspect_validity = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_valid_suspects
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
		if = {
			limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_suspect = yes }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_valid_suspects = THIS }
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect_cleared }
	}
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
	for_each_scope_loop = { array = secret_alliance_valid_suspects add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_suspects = THIS } }
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_valid_suspects
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope
				var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = { NOT = { secret_alliance_is_valid_suspect = yes } }
			}
			clear_variable = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_rebuild_visible_suspects = yes
}

secret_alliance_maybe_recruit_member = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_i_member_cap }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_member_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_member_cap }
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_major = no
				num_of_factories < constant:secret_alliance_event.weak_target_factory_threshold
				num_divisions < constant:secret_alliance_event.weak_target_division_threshold
			}
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap = constant:secret_alliance_event.weak_target_member_cap_penalty }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_generated_tension > constant:secret_alliance_founder_weight.medium_tension_threshold } }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap = constant:secret_alliance_event.peaceful_target_member_cap_penalty }
		}
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_current_member_cap min = constant:secret_alliance_event.dynamic_member_cap_floor max = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_member_cap }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_available_member_ceiling = global.secret_alliance_member_count }
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes
				secret_alliance_is_valid_human_recruit = yes
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_available_member_ceiling = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap > secret_alliance_available_member_ceiling } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_member_cap = secret_alliance_available_member_ceiling }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < secret_alliance_current_member_cap }
		}
		secret_alliance_schedule_minor_invitation = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_maybe_run_internal_dispute = {
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_dispute_pool
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes }
				add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_dispute_pool = THIS }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_dispute_pool^num > 1 } }
			random_scope_in_array = {
				array = secret_alliance_dispute_pool
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_dispute_member_a
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_dispute_participant
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_dispute_commitment_a = secret_alliance_private_commitment }
			}
			random_scope_in_array = {
				array = secret_alliance_dispute_pool
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_dispute_participant } }
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_dispute_member_b
				set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_dispute_commitment_b = secret_alliance_private_commitment }
			}
			event_target:secret_alliance_dispute_member_a = { clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_dispute_participant }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_dispute_commitment_a = global.secret_alliance_dispute_commitment_b } }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_compatible_cohesion }
				event_target:secret_alliance_dispute_member_a = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_settlement_commitment } secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes }
				event_target:secret_alliance_dispute_member_b = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_settlement_commitment } secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_cohesion }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_readiness }
				event_target:secret_alliance_dispute_member_a = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_failure_commitment } secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes }
				event_target:secret_alliance_dispute_member_b = {
					add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_failure_commitment }
					secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.security_restraint } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.punitive } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.claim_settlement } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.regime_pressure } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.political_change } }
					else = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.postwar_gain } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_dispute_memory_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.dispute_memory_days }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_dispute_agenda_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.dispute_agenda_delay_days }
			set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute days = secret_alliance_dispute_memory_days }
			set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_agenda_delayed_by_dispute days = secret_alliance_dispute_agenda_days }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.12 } }
			clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_dispute_member_a
			clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_dispute_member_b
		}
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_dispute_pool
}

secret_alliance_maybe_run_leak = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i }
		secret_alliance_select_true_member_clue = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_leak_pause_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.major_leak_pause_days }
		set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recent_leak days = secret_alliance_leak_pause_days }
		set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recruitment_paused_by_leak days = secret_alliance_leak_pause_days }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.13 } }
	}
}

secret_alliance_maybe_run_defection = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion < constant:secret_alliance_band.cohesion_uneasy } }
		secret_alliance_select_defecting_member = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_select_defecting_member = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_defection_pool
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor }
			}
			secret_alliance_add_current_defection_tickets = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_defection_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_defection_pool
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_defected
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
			clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			subtract_from_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.defection_cohesion }
				secret_alliance_register_current_country_as_suspect = yes
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_likely }
			save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_defecting_member
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.human }
		event_target:secret_alliance_defecting_member = { save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source }
		secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.14 } }
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_defection_pool
}

secret_alliance_launch_weighted_operation = {
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_operation_active
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_surface_available = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_surface_available = 0 }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					impassable = no
					OR = { industrial_complex > 0 infrastructure > 0 }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_surface_available = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_neighbor_country = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_surface_available = 1 }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.diplomatic_family }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.intelligence_family }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.sabotage_family }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.political_family }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.military_family }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recruitment_family }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.liaison } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_role } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.intelligence } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_role } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_role } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_role } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_role } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.fear } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes OR = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.ideology } check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.regime_survival } } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.patronage } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_motive } }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.punitive } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.regime_pressure } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } }
	else = { add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.matching_doctrine } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recent_family } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recent_family } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recent_family } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recent_family } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recent_family } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.recent_family } }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } } ROOT = { add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } } ROOT = { add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } } ROOT = { add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } } }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_industrial_state } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } }
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_stockpile_state } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } }
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_border_state } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } }
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_port_airfield_state } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prepared_surface } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.alertness_high }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.alertness_high }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.alertness_high }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.alertness_high }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness > constant:secret_alliance_band.readiness_operational } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.readiness_high }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.readiness_high }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.readiness_high }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_exposed_operation_surface }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_exposed_surface_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.exposed_surface } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_exposed_surface_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.exposed_surface } }
	}
	secret_alliance_apply_prior_operation_result_weight = yes
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_political_family_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_military_family_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.maximum }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.minimum max = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.maximum }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = 0 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_surface_available < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_military_surface_available < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = 0 } }
	random_list = {
		secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.external_access }
		}
		secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.communications }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.intelligence_access }
		}
		secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.method }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.logistics_access }
		}
		secret_alliance_political_family_weight = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.financial }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.political_access }
		}
		secret_alliance_military_family_weight = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.military }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.military_access }
		}
		secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.human }
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.recruitment_access }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_low }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_alert } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_guarded }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_high }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_mobilized } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_desperate }
	}

	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
					is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_under_cover }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.liaison }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.intelligence }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border }
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment }
						OR = {
							check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.liaison }
							check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			secret_alliance_add_current_operation_actor_tickets = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool^num < 1 } }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_under_cover }
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
						is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					}
				}
				secret_alliance_add_current_operation_actor_tickets = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool^num < 1 } }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
						is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					}
				}
				secret_alliance_add_current_operation_actor_tickets = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool
			save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_actor
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_operation_actor
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_operation_actor_pool
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_supporter
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor }
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_operation_support_pool
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_operation_actor }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_under_cover }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_operation_support_pool = THIS }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_support_pool^num > 0 } }
			random_scope_in_array = {
				array = secret_alliance_operation_support_pool
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_supporter
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_operation_supporter
			}
		}
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_operation_support_pool
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { impassable = no OR = { industrial_complex > 0 infrastructure > 0 } }
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_surface
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { impassable = no any_neighbor_state = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor } }
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_surface
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { impassable = no OR = { is_coastal = yes air_base > 0 } }
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_surface
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_continuity_state }
			event_target:secret_alliance_protected_continuity_state = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_surface }
		}
		else = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_operation_surface } } }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recovery_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.recovery_days }
	set_global_flag = {
		flag = secret_alliance_operation_recovery
		days = secret_alliance_recovery_days
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.9 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.6 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.7 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii }
			random_list = {
				constant:secret_alliance_operation.political_pressure_operation_weight = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.10 } } }
				constant:secret_alliance_operation.political_violence_operation_weight = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.21 } } }
			}
		}
		else = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.10 } } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.8 } }
	}
	else = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.11 } } }
}

secret_alliance_apply_prior_operation_result_weight = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prior_result_weight = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.full } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prior_result_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prior_full_success } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.partial } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prior_result_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prior_partial_trace } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.failure } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_prior_result_weight = constant:secret_alliance_operation_weight.prior_disruption } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_diplomatic_family_weight = secret_alliance_prior_result_weight } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_intelligence_family_weight = secret_alliance_prior_result_weight } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_sabotage_family_weight = secret_alliance_prior_result_weight } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_political_family_weight = secret_alliance_prior_result_weight } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_military_family_weight = secret_alliance_prior_result_weight } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recruitment_family_weight = secret_alliance_prior_result_weight } }
}

secret_alliance_register_current_country_as_suspect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect } }
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect
		add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_suspects = THIS }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.secret_alliance_suspect_cleanup_archive value = THIS } } }
			add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_suspect_cleanup_archive = THIS }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope } }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = PREV } }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_rebuild_visible_suspects = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_visible_suspect
	}
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_slots = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_searching = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_slots < constant:secret_alliance_operation.visible_suspect_cap }
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_searching > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_best_confidence = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_best_recency = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_searching = 0 }
		clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_visible_candidate
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect_cleared }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_visible_suspect }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence > secret_alliance_visible_best_confidence }
						AND = {
							check_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence = secret_alliance_visible_best_confidence }
							check_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_recency > secret_alliance_visible_best_recency }
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_best_confidence = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence }
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_best_recency = secret_alliance_suspect_recency }
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_visible_candidate
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_searching = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_visible_candidate }
			event_target:secret_alliance_visible_candidate = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_visible_suspect
				add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects = THIS }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_visible_slots = 1 }
		}
	}
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_visible_candidate
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { secret_alliance_refresh_gui_card_frames = yes }
}

secret_alliance_refresh_gui_card_frames = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_1_frame = 0 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_2_frame = 0 }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_3_frame = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^num > 0 } }
		if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^0 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_confirmed compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_1_frame = 3 } }
		else_if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^0 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_likely compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_1_frame = 2 } }
		else_if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^0 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_possible compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_1_frame = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^num > 1 } }
		if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^1 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_confirmed compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_2_frame = 3 } }
		else_if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^1 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_likely compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_2_frame = 2 } }
		else_if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^1 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_possible compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_2_frame = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^num > 2 } }
		if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^2 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_confirmed compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_3_frame = 3 } }
		else_if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^2 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_likely compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_3_frame = 2 } }
		else_if = { limit = { global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects^2 = { check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_possible compare = greater_than_or_equals } } } set_variable = { secret_alliance_gui_card_3_frame = 1 } }
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_new_clue = {
	# Each country keeps its own independent-class count. The global count is a
	# dossier-wide measure and is never used as a substitute for corroborating a
	# named suspect.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source > 0 } }
		event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = {
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_evidence_classes = 1 }
			set_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_recency = global.secret_alliance_operation_pulses }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class > 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_independent_evidence_classes = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.independent_class }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.additional_source_in_class }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_confidence_gain = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.confidence_independent_class }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_evidence_classes > 1 } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_confidence_gain = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.confidence_corroboration }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.cross_class_corroboration }
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { secret_alliance_raise_current_suspect_confidence = yes }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_clue_registered
	}
	else = { set_global_flag = secret_alliance_duplicate_clue_rejected }
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	secret_alliance_rebuild_visible_suspects = yes
}

secret_alliance_close_current_weak_lead = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_lead_source
			NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence < constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_likely }
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect_cleared
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_visible_suspect
		remove_from_array = { array = global.secret_alliance_suspects value = THIS }
		remove_from_array = { array = global.secret_alliance_visible_suspects value = THIS }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.cleared_lead_credibility_recovery }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.cleared_lead_stability }
		secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
		secret_alliance_rebuild_visible_suspects = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.method }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_method_source } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 1 }
			event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_method_source }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_method } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_method
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.communications }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_communications_source } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 1 }
			event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_communications_source }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_communications } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_communications
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.financial }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_financial_source } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 1 }
			event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_financial_source }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_financial } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_financial
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_diplomatic_source } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 1 }
			event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_diplomatic_source }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_diplomatic } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_diplomatic
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.military }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_military_source } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 1 }
			event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_military_source }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_military } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_military
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.human }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_human_source } } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source = 1 }
			event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_human_source }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_human } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evidence_class_human
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_class = 1 }
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_apply_new_clue = yes
}

secret_alliance_raise_current_suspect_confidence = {
	# Input: temp variable secret_alliance_confidence_gain.
	secret_alliance_register_current_country_as_suspect = yes
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_suspect_confidence = secret_alliance_confidence_gain }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_confidence value = constant:secret_alliance_band.confidence_confirmed compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_suspect_evidence_classes value = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.name_member_classes compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_confirmed_by_target
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_confirmed_registry_member } }
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_confirmed_registry_member
			add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_confirmed_members = THIS }
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_true_member_confirmed
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_founder } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_true_founder_confirmed
			}
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_rebuild_visible_suspects = yes
}

secret_alliance_select_true_member_clue = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.communications }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } }
