		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.diplomatic }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.communications }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.method }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.financial }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.military }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = constant:secret_alliance_evidence_class.human }
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_true_member_clue_pool
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes }
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_true_member_clue_pool = THIS }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_true_member_clue_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_true_member_clue_pool
			save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source
			secret_alliance_register_current_country_as_suspect = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source }
		secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_true_member_clue_pool
}

secret_alliance_select_operation_suspect = {
	random_list = {
		constant:secret_alliance_action_value.true_clue_weight = { secret_alliance_select_true_member_clue = yes }
		constant:secret_alliance_action_value.false_clue_weight = {
			clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_innocent_suspect_pool
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					is_ai = yes
					uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes
					NOT = { is_special_chaos_country = yes }
					NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect_cleared }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_innocent_suspect_pool = THIS }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_innocent_suspect_pool^num > 0 } }
				random_scope_in_array = {
					array = secret_alliance_innocent_suspect_pool
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_lead_source
					save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source
				}
				secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
			}
			clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_innocent_suspect_pool
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_operation_minor_success = {
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.partial }
	secret_alliance_prepare_operation_risk_resolution = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.readiness_minor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = secret_alliance_operation_risk_readiness_bonus }
	secret_alliance_change_named_operation_readiness = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_success_cohesion }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor }
			event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor = {
				add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_minor_success_commitment }
				add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change }
				secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
			}
		}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_supporter }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_supporter = {
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.coordinated_support_commitment }
			secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.coordinated_readiness }
	}
	secret_alliance_register_operation_trace = yes
	secret_alliance_apply_operation_surface_success = yes
	secret_alliance_finish_operation_dossier = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_operation_major_success = {
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.full }
	secret_alliance_prepare_operation_risk_resolution = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.readiness_major }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = secret_alliance_operation_risk_readiness_bonus }
	secret_alliance_change_named_operation_readiness = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_success_cohesion }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.war_pressure_major }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor }
			event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor = {
				add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_major_success_commitment }
				add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change }
				secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
			}
		}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_supporter }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_supporter = {
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.coordinated_support_commitment }
			secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.coordinated_readiness }
	}
	secret_alliance_apply_full_operation_preparedness_test = yes
	secret_alliance_apply_operation_surface_success = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_recovery_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.severe_recovery_days }
	set_global_flag = {
		flag = secret_alliance_operation_recovery
		days = secret_alliance_recovery_days
	}
	secret_alliance_finish_operation_dossier = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_operation_disrupted = {
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.failure }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.disrupted_readiness }
	secret_alliance_change_named_operation_readiness = yes
	secret_alliance_prepare_operation_disruption_risk_resolution = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_failure_cohesion }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_failure_alertness }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor = {
			save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_disrupted_commitment }
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change }
			secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
		secret_alliance_apply_operation_risk_clue_bonus = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_supporter }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_supporter = {
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment_score = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.coordinated_disruption_commitment }
			secret_alliance_refresh_current_member_commitment = yes
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_preserve_disrupted_operation_surface = yes
	secret_alliance_finish_operation_dossier = yes
}

secret_alliance_register_operation_trace = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_class = global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor = { save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_clue_source }
		secret_alliance_register_operation_clue = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_change_named_operation_readiness = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.external_access } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.external_access_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.intelligence_access } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.intelligence_access_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.logistics_access } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.logistics_access_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.political_access } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.political_access_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer = constant:secret_alliance_readiness_layer.military_access } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.military_access_factor }
	}
	else = {
		multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.recruitment_access_factor }
	}
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = secret_alliance_operation_readiness_delta }
}

secret_alliance_prepare_operation_risk_resolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_readiness_bonus = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_desperate } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_readiness_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.desperate_success_readiness_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.desperate_success_commitment_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_high } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_readiness_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.high_success_readiness_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.high_success_commitment_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_guarded } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_readiness_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.guarded_success_readiness_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.guarded_success_commitment_bonus }
	}
}

secret_alliance_prepare_operation_disruption_risk_resolution = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_evidence_bonus = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_confidence_bonus = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_desperate } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.desperate_disruption_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_evidence_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.desperate_clue_evidence_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_confidence_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.desperate_clue_confidence_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_high } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.high_disruption_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_evidence_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.high_clue_evidence_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_confidence_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.high_clue_confidence_bonus }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_guarded } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_commitment_change = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.guarded_disruption_commitment }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_evidence_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.guarded_clue_evidence_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_confidence_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.guarded_clue_confidence_bonus }
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_operation_risk_clue_bonus = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { secret_alliance_clue_new_source > 0 }
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_clue_source
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_evidence_bonus }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_confidence_gain = secret_alliance_operation_risk_clue_confidence_bonus }
		event_target:secret_alliance_clue_source = { secret_alliance_raise_current_suspect_confidence = yes }
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_operation_surface_success = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage }
		}
		secret_alliance_check_current_operation_surface_protection = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected < 1 }
			}
			event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_desperate } }
					damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.surface_damage_desperate }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_high } }
					damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.surface_damage_high }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_risk = constant:secret_alliance_operation.operation_risk_guarded } }
					damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.surface_damage_guarded }
				}
				else = { damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.surface_damage_low } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected > 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.prepared_surface_readiness_denial }
			secret_alliance_preserve_disrupted_operation_surface = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation }
		}
		secret_alliance_check_current_operation_surface_protection = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected > 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.prepared_surface_readiness_denial }
			secret_alliance_preserve_disrupted_operation_surface = yes
		}
		else = {
			clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_mapped_military_surface
			event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_mapped_military_surface }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface }
		secret_alliance_check_current_operation_surface_protection = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected > 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.prepared_surface_readiness_denial }
			secret_alliance_preserve_disrupted_operation_surface = yes
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_check_current_operation_surface_protection = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } }
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_industrial_state event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { state = event_target:secret_alliance_protected_industrial_state } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_stockpile_state event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { state = event_target:secret_alliance_protected_stockpile_state } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } }
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_border_state event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { state = event_target:secret_alliance_protected_border_state } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } }
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_port_airfield_state event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { state = event_target:secret_alliance_protected_port_airfield_state } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure }
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment }
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_continuity_state event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { state = event_target:secret_alliance_protected_continuity_state } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_full_operation_preparedness_test = {
	secret_alliance_check_current_operation_surface_protection = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_component_damage = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.unprotected_component_damage }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_surface_protected > 0 } } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_operation_component_damage = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.protected_component_damage } }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.diplomatic_isolation } has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied_consultation }
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_allied_consultation = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage }
			clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied_consultation min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.intelligence_penetration } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_ciphers } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_ciphers = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage } clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_ciphers min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap } }
			else_if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_plans } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_plans = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage } clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_plans min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial_chokepoints } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial_chokepoints = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage } clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial_chokepoints min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap } }
			if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_stockpiles } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_stockpiles = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage } clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_stockpiles min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.political_social_pressure } has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_cabinet }
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_cabinet = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage }
			clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_cabinet min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation } has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_communications }
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border_communications = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage }
			clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_communications min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.recruitment } has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_sites_source }
			add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_sites_source = secret_alliance_operation_component_damage }
			clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_sites_source min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
		}
		secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_preserve_disrupted_operation_surface = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface
			OR = {
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.industrial_transport_sabotage }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation }
			}
		}
		clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_exposed_operation_surface
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_surface = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_exposed_operation_surface }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_exposed_surface_family = global.secret_alliance_operation_family }
	}
}

secret_alliance_finish_operation_dossier = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor = { clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_operation_actor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_supporter }
		event_target:secret_alliance_operation_supporter = { clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_current_operation_supporter }
	}
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_recent_operation_family = global.secret_alliance_operation_family }
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_operation_active
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_supporter
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_surface
	clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_operation_risk
	clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_operation_evidence_class
	clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_operation_readiness_layer
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_prepare_liaison_objective = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_liaison_objective_verified
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_liaison_suspect_scope
	capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_liaison_objective_state }
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor } set_variable = { secret_alliance_liaison_suspect_scope = event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope } set_variable = { secret_alliance_liaison_suspect_scope = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope } }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_mission_verify_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.watch_liaison_verify_days }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.54 days = secret_alliance_mission_verify_days }
}

secret_alliance_prepare_courier_objective = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_courier_objective_verified
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_courier_suspect_scope
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_courier_objective_state
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			impassable = no
			OR = {
				is_coastal = yes
				any_neighbor_state = { NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_courier_objective_state
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_courier_objective_state } } capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_courier_objective_state } }
	if = { limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_operation_actor } set_variable = { secret_alliance_courier_suspect_scope = event_target:secret_alliance_operation_actor } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope } set_variable = { secret_alliance_courier_suspect_scope = secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope } }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_mission_verify_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.seize_courier_verify_days }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.55 days = secret_alliance_mission_verify_days }
}

# Delayed verification prevents an objective from completing in the same tick in
# which it is activated. Each check is tied to the named state and to the
# protection or field action described by the mission.
secret_alliance_verify_liaison_objective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_active_mission = secret_alliance_watch_liaison_route
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_liaison_objective_state
			event_target:secret_alliance_liaison_objective_state = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_liaison_watch_active
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_ciphers_rotated
			NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_recent_source_loss }
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_liaison_objective_verified
	}
	else_if = { limit = { has_active_mission = secret_alliance_watch_liaison_route } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_mission_verify_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.watch_liaison_verify_days } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.54 days = secret_alliance_mission_verify_days } }
}

secret_alliance_verify_courier_objective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_active_mission = secret_alliance_seize_compromised_courier
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_courier_objective_state
			event_target:secret_alliance_courier_objective_state = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			divisions_in_state = { state = event_target:secret_alliance_courier_objective_state size > 0 }
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_routes_mapped
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_courier_objective_verified
	}
	else_if = { limit = { has_active_mission = secret_alliance_seize_compromised_courier } set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_mission_verify_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.seize_courier_verify_days } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.55 days = secret_alliance_mission_verify_days } }
}

			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_ciphers_rotated
			NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_recent_source_loss }

	}

	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_spend = -1 }
	add_command_power = secret_alliance_spend
}

secret_alliance_release_emergency_commitment = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_active_emergency > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { secret_alliance_active_emergency = 1 } }
}

secret_alliance_release_diplomacy_commitment = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_active_diplomacy > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { secret_alliance_active_diplomacy = 1 } }
}

secret_alliance_release_offensive_commitment = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_active_offensive > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { secret_alliance_active_offensive = 1 } }
}

secret_alliance_pay_war_cost = {
	secret_alliance_pay_command_power = yes
	secret_alliance_pay_support_equipment = yes
	secret_alliance_pay_fuel = yes
}

secret_alliance_record_full_success = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_last_outcome_band = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.full }
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_full_successes = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.alertness_minor }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_known_plans_gain = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.preparedness_medium }
	secret_alliance_add_known_plans_component = yes
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_record_partial_success = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_last_outcome_band = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.partial }
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_partial_successes = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.alertness_minor }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_known_plans_gain = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.preparedness_minor }
	secret_alliance_add_known_plans_component = yes
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_add_known_plans_component = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_known_plans_component_active } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_known_plans_days = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.known_plans_days }
		set_country_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_known_plans_component_active days = secret_alliance_known_plans_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.49 days = secret_alliance_known_plans_days }
	}
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_known_plans = secret_alliance_known_plans_gain }
	clamp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_preparedness_known_plans
		min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap
	}
	secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes
}

secret_alliance_record_failure = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_last_outcome_band = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.failure }
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_failures = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.alertness_major }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.operation_success_cohesion }
	secret_alliance_select_operation_suspect = yes
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_recent_source_loss
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_resolve_weighted_counter_network_action = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_full_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.base_full }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_partial_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.base_partial }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_failure_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.base_failure }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence > constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_partial_dossier } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_full_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.evidence_bonus }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_failure_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.evidence_failure_reduction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_full_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.preparedness_bonus }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_failure_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.preparedness_failure_reduction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_full_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.alertness_penalty }
		add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_failure_chance = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.alertness_penalty }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_full_chance min = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.full_min max = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.full_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_partial_chance min = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.partial_min max = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.partial_max }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_failure_chance min = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.failure_min max = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.failure_max }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_outcome_total = secret_alliance_full_chance }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_outcome_total = secret_alliance_partial_chance }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_outcome_total = secret_alliance_failure_chance }
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = secret_alliance_outcome_roll
		min = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.random_roll_min
		max = secret_alliance_outcome_total
		integer = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_partial_upper = secret_alliance_full_chance }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_partial_upper = secret_alliance_partial_chance }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = secret_alliance_outcome_roll
				value = secret_alliance_full_chance
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		secret_alliance_select_true_member_clue = yes
		secret_alliance_record_full_success = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = secret_alliance_outcome_roll
				value = secret_alliance_partial_upper
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		secret_alliance_select_operation_suspect = yes
		secret_alliance_record_partial_success = yes
	}
	else = {
		secret_alliance_record_failure = yes
	}
}

	}
}

secret_alliance_expire_investigation_mission = {
	secret_alliance_cancel_one_investigation = yes
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_last_outcome_band = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.expired }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_partial_chance = constant:secret_alliance_mission.expiry_partial_base_chance }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_component = global.secret_alliance_preparedness }
	divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_component = constant:secret_alliance_mission.expiry_preparedness_divisor }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_partial_chance = secret_alliance_expiry_component }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_component = global.secret_alliance_alertness }
	divide_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_component = constant:secret_alliance_mission.expiry_alertness_divisor }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_expiry_partial_chance = secret_alliance_expiry_component }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_expiry_partial_chance
		min = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.expiry_partial_min
		max = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_chance.expiry_partial_max
	}
	random = {
		chance = secret_alliance_expiry_partial_chance
		secret_alliance_record_partial_success = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_last_outcome_band = constant:secret_alliance_outcome_band.partial } } }
		secret_alliance_record_failure = yes
	}
}

# Dynamic limited border conflict. This follows the Event 008 verified pattern:
# store state IDs in country variables, pass them through state = var:, and do
# not transfer either state when the clash ends.
secret_alliance_prepare_dynamic_border_pair = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_border_attacker_state_found = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_border_defender_state_found = 0 }
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			impassable = no
			NOT = { has_border_war = yes }
			any_neighbor_state = {
				impassable = no
				NOT = { has_border_war = yes }
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope
			}
		}
		set_variable = { ROOT.secret_alliance_border_attacker_state = id }
		ROOT = { set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_border_attacker_state_found = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_border_attacker_state_found > 0 } }
		var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					impassable = no
					NOT = { has_border_war = yes }
					any_neighbor_state = { state = var:ROOT.secret_alliance_border_attacker_state }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.secret_alliance_border_defender_state = id }
				ROOT = { set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_border_defender_state_found = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_border_defender_state_found > 0 } }
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_pair_prepared
	}
}

secret_alliance_reinforce_prepared_border_pair = {
	secret_alliance_pay_offensive_cost = yes
	secret_alliance_prepare_dynamic_border_pair = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_pair_prepared }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_patrol_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.offensive_commitment_days }
		set_country_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_reinforced_border_patrols days = secret_alliance_patrol_days }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border_patrols = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.border_gain }
		secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.68 days = secret_alliance_patrol_days }
	}
}

secret_alliance_start_dynamic_border_conflict = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_pair_prepared } }
		secret_alliance_prepare_dynamic_border_pair = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_pair_prepared
			secret_alliance_prepared_border_pair_has_units = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_pay_offensive_cost = yes
		secret_alliance_pay_manpower = yes
		start_border_war = {
			change_state_after_war = no
			combat_width = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.combat_width
			minimum_duration_in_days = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.minimum_duration_days
			attacker = {
				state = var:secret_alliance_border_attacker_state
				num_provinces = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.provinces
				on_win = chaosx.nr11.30
				on_lose = chaosx.nr11.31
				on_cancel = chaosx.nr11.32
				modifier = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.attacker_modifier
			}
			defender = {
				state = var:secret_alliance_border_defender_state
				num_provinces = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.provinces
				on_win = chaosx.nr11.31
				on_lose = chaosx.nr11.30
				on_cancel = chaosx.nr11.32
				modifier = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.defender_modifier
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_active
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_chain_active
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_unresolved
		add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_active_border = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.war_pressure_major }
	}
}

secret_alliance_clear_border_conflict_runtime = {
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_unresolved
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_active
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_border_pair_prepared
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_active_border > 0 } } subtract_from_variable = { secret_alliance_active_border = 1 } }
		clear_variable = secret_alliance_border_attacker_state
		clear_variable = secret_alliance_border_defender_state
	}
}

secret_alliance_cancel_exact_border_conflict = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_unresolved
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				has_variable = secret_alliance_border_attacker_state
				has_variable = secret_alliance_border_defender_state
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_silent_border_cancel
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			cancel_border_war = {
				attacker = var:secret_alliance_border_attacker_state
				defender = var:secret_alliance_border_defender_state
			}
		}
		secret_alliance_clear_border_conflict_runtime = yes
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_silent_border_cancel
	}
}

secret_alliance_resolve_border_conflict_win = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_unresolved }
		secret_alliance_clear_border_conflict_runtime = yes
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_operation_family = constant:secret_alliance_operation_family.military_preparation }
		secret_alliance_select_true_member_clue = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.border_conflict_readiness_loss }
		secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_resolve_border_conflict_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_unresolved }
		secret_alliance_clear_border_conflict_runtime = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.border_conflict_recruitment }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.border_conflict_pressure }
		secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_resolve_border_conflict_cancel = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_border_conflict_unresolved NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_silent_border_cancel } }
		secret_alliance_clear_border_conflict_runtime = yes
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.war_pressure_minor }
		secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_escalate_border_conflict = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_border_escalation_war_support_payment = secret_alliance_cost_border_escalation_war_support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_border_escalation_war_support_payment = -1 }
	add_war_support = secret_alliance_border_escalation_war_support_payment
	secret_alliance_cancel_exact_border_conflict = yes
	var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_war_anchor }
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
	declare_war_on = {
		target = var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope
		type = topple_government
	}
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.preemption }
		secret_alliance_reveal_pact = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_withdraw_border_conflict = {
	secret_alliance_cancel_exact_border_conflict = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.withdraw_pressure_change }
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_negotiate_border_conflict = {
	secret_alliance_pay_diplomacy_cost = yes
	secret_alliance_cancel_exact_border_conflict = yes
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.negotiated_pressure_change }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_border_conflict.negotiated_cohesion_change }
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_public_conference_reveal = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.public_conference }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion < constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.fracture_cohesion }
					has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute
					secret_alliance_has_turned_channel = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.fractured }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_evidence value = constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.forced_evidence compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					any_country = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_at_entry has_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_exposed }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.public_dossier }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion > constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.controlled_cohesion }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness > constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.controlled_readiness }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness < constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded }
			}
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_controlled_public_reveal
		}
		else = {
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_weakened_public_reveal
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.weakened }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.weakened_readiness_loss }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.weakened_cohesion_loss }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.fractured } }
			secret_alliance_mark_public_reveal_fracture = yes
		}
		secret_alliance_reveal_pact = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_mark_public_reveal_fracture = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_public_fracture_pool
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_hidden_member = yes
				NOT = { is_major = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member }
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment < constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_public_fracture_pool = THIS }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_public_fracture_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = { array = secret_alliance_public_fracture_pool set_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member }
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_public_fracture_pool
}

secret_alliance_resolve_coalition_countdown = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_countdown_delay_count < constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.countdown_delay_cap }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure < constant:secret_alliance_band.war_pressure_war }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_countdown_delay_count = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.countdown_delay_readiness_loss }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.countdown_delay_pressure_loss }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_countdown_delayed_by_defenses
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_countdown_restart_delay_days = constant:secret_alliance_evolution_iii_state.countdown_restart_delay_days }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.70 days = secret_alliance_countdown_restart_delay_days } }
		secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	}
	else = { secret_alliance_public_conference_reveal = yes }
}

secret_alliance_restart_coalition_countdown = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii }
		activate_mission = secret_alliance_coalition_countdown
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PROTECTION, DIPLOMACY, DECEPTION, EXPOSURE, AND EMERGENCY HELPERS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = {
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_plans } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_plans } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_ciphers } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff_ciphers } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_staff } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_staff } }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial_chokepoints } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial_chokepoints } }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_transport = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_stockpiles } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_transport = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_stockpiles } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_ports } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_transport = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport_ports } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_transport } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_transport = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_transport } }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_communications } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_communications } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_patrols } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_patrols } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_border } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_border } }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_cabinet } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_cabinet } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_sites_source } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity_sites_source } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_continuity } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_continuity } }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_allied = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied_consultation } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_allied = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied_consultation } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_alliance } add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_allied = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_alliance } }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_border min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
	clamp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.component_cap }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_baseline } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_baseline } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_staff } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_transport } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_border } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_border } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_known_plans } add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = secret_alliance_preparedness_known_plans } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > 0 } }
		add_ideas = secret_alliance_hardened_networks
	}
	else = { remove_ideas = secret_alliance_hardened_networks }
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_begin_preparedness_project = {
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_active_protections = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.alertness_minor }
	secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes
}

secret_alliance_clear_protected_state_targets = {
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_industrial_state
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_stockpile_state
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_border_state
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_port_airfield_state
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_protected_continuity_state

secret_alliance_apply_false_accusation_consequences = {
	# Current scope is the fixed target; selected suspect is global.
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_accusation_made
	set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_innocent_named
	add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_false_accusation_count = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_false_accusation_credibility_loss = secret_alliance_false_accusation_count }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_false_accusation_credibility_loss = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.false_accusation_credibility_loss }
	subtract_from_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = secret_alliance_false_accusation_credibility_loss }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_scale = secret_alliance_false_accusation_count }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_scale = 1 }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_scale min = 0 max = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.repeated_accusation_cap }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_cohesion = secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.repeated_accusation_cohesion }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_alertness = secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.repeated_accusation_alertness }
	add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.false_accusation_stability
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.false_accusation_cohesion }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_cohesion }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = constant:secret_alliance_operation_value.alertness_major }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = secret_alliance_repeated_accusation_alertness }
	var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = {
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_publicly_named
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_innocent_accused
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			modifier = large_decrease
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_lead_source
				secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
			}
			secret_alliance_register_minor_member = yes
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_recruited_by_false_accusation
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

	}

secret_alliance_attempt_selected_member_defection = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_selected_suspect_is_true_member = yes }
		var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment < constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.fear }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism }
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_defected
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
				clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				subtract_from_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 1 }
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.defection_cohesion }
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.dispute_cohesion }
			}
		}
		secret_alliance_confirm_selected_true_member = yes
	}
	else = {
		secret_alliance_apply_false_accusation_consequences = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_emergency_preempt_coalition = {
	secret_alliance_pay_emergency_cost = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_preemption_war_support_payment = secret_alliance_cost_preemption_war_support }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_preemption_war_support_payment = -1 }
	add_war_support = secret_alliance_preemption_war_support_payment
	if = {
		limit = { var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member } } }
		add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.preemption_false_case_stability
		set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_innocent_attacked
		add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_false_accusation_count = 1 }
	}
	var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_war_anchor }
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
	declare_war_on = {
		target = var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope
		type = topple_government
	}
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.preemption }
		secret_alliance_reveal_pact = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_emergency_seek_defensive_alliance = {
	secret_alliance_pay_emergency_cost = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_emergency_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.emergency_commitment_days }
	set_country_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_defensive_alliance_sought days = secret_alliance_emergency_days }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_alliance = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.defensive_alliance_gain }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.defensive_alliance_pressure }
	secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.64 days = secret_alliance_emergency_days }
}

secret_alliance_emergency_evacuate_leadership = {
	secret_alliance_pay_emergency_cost = yes
	secret_alliance_pay_trains = yes
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_emergency_days = constant:secret_alliance_mission.emergency_commitment_days }
	set_country_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_leadership_evacuated days = secret_alliance_emergency_days }
	add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.leadership_evacuation_stability
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_continuity = constant:secret_alliance_preparedness.leadership_evacuation_continuity_gain }
	secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.65 days = secret_alliance_emergency_days }
}

secret_alliance_end_emergency_staff_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_emergency_mobilized } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_staff } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }
secret_alliance_end_emergency_border_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_exposed_approaches_fortified } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_border } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }
secret_alliance_end_emergency_transport_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_overseas_assets_recalled } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_transport } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }
secret_alliance_end_emergency_alliance_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_defensive_alliance_sought } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_alliance } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }
secret_alliance_end_emergency_continuity_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_leadership_evacuated } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_emergency_continuity } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }
secret_alliance_end_border_patrol_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reinforced_border_patrols } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_border_patrols } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }
secret_alliance_end_allied_consultation_preparedness = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_allied_consultation } } clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_allied_consultation } secret_alliance_recalculate_preparedness = yes }

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# REVEAL, FACTION FORMATION, WAR CONVERGENCE, AND AI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

secret_alliance_consider_current_reveal_leader_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_base }
	if = { limit = { num_divisions > constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_division_low } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_low_score } }
	if = { limit = { num_divisions > constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_division_medium } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_medium_score } }
	if = { limit = { num_divisions > constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_division_high } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_high_score } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_factory_low } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_low_score } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_factory_medium } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_medium_score } }
	if = { limit = { num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_factory_high } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score = constant:secret_alliance_ai.leader_strength_high_score } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score > secret_alliance_best_leader_score } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_best_leader_score = secret_alliance_candidate_leader_score }
		save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_leader
	}
}

secret_alliance_select_reveal_leader = {
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_leader

	# The first accepted major sponsor is the designated public leader. A later
	# sponsor cannot displace it while it remains an active valid member.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major
			event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor
				is_major = yes
			}
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_leader }
	}

	# If the designated sponsor is gone, choose the strongest remaining major.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_leader } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_best_leader_score = 0 }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
					is_major = yes
				}
				secret_alliance_consider_current_reveal_leader_candidate = yes
			}
		}
	}

	# With no active major, preserve founder ownership and rank founders only by
	# their current military and industrial capacity.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_leader } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_best_leader_score = 0 }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_founder
				}
				secret_alliance_consider_current_reveal_leader_candidate = yes
			}
		}
	}

	# A hostile-war trigger is the final valid leader only when no sponsor,
	# active major, or surviving founder can take the chair.
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_leader }
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_war_anchor
			event_target:secret_alliance_war_anchor = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			}
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_war_anchor = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_leader }
	}
}

secret_alliance_assign_preparedness_delayed_calls = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.hostile_war } }
		for_each_scope_loop = { array = global.secret_alliance_members clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_preparedness_threshold }
		}
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
					NOT = { is_major = yes }
					NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_leader }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member }
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment < constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_tickets = 1 }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.tentative } } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_tickets = 2 } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.intelligence } event_target:secret_alliance_target = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_staff > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_component_threshold } } }
							AND = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics } event_target:secret_alliance_target = { OR = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_industrial > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_component_threshold } check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_transport > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_component_threshold } } } }
							AND = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border } event_target:secret_alliance_target = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_border > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_component_threshold } } }
							AND = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political } event_target:secret_alliance_target = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_continuity > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_component_threshold } } }
							AND = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.liaison } event_target:secret_alliance_target = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_preparedness_allied > constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_component_threshold } } }
						}
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_tickets = 2 }
				}
				while_loop_effect = {
					limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_tickets > 0 } }
					add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool = THIS }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_tickets = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_selection_count = 0 }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool^num > 0 }
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_selection_count < constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_cap }
			}
			random_scope_in_array = {
				array = secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call
				save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_delayed_call_selection
			}
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { is_in_array = { array = secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool value = event_target:secret_alliance_delayed_call_selection } }
				remove_from_temp_array = { array = secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool value = event_target:secret_alliance_delayed_call_selection }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_selection_count = 1 }
		}
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_delayed_call_pool
	}
}

secret_alliance_snapshot_reveal_state = {
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_member_count = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_major_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_all_members_confirmed_snapshot = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_turned_founder_or_major_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_turned_channel_preserved_snapshot = 0 }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			}
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_member_count = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { is_major = yes }
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_major_count = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_confirmed_by_target } }
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_all_members_confirmed_snapshot = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_founder
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_at_entry
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_turned_founder_or_major_snapshot = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_channel_preserved }
					set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_turned_channel_preserved_snapshot = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_reveal_member_count_snapshot = global.secret_alliance_reveal_member_count }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_reveal_major_count_snapshot = global.secret_alliance_reveal_major_count }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_evidence_snapshot = global.secret_alliance_evidence }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_independent_evidence_classes_snapshot = global.secret_alliance_independent_evidence_classes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_turned_founder_or_major_snapshot > 0 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_turned_founder_or_major_snapshot
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_turned_channel_preserved_snapshot > 0 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_turned_channel_preserved_snapshot
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_all_members_confirmed_snapshot > 0 } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_all_members_confirmed
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = constant:secret_alliance_band.evidence_complete_network }
				check_variable = {
					var = global.secret_alliance_independent_evidence_classes
					value = constant:secret_alliance_evidence.complete_network_classes
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_all_members_confirmed_snapshot > 0 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_innocent_named }
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_every_thread_ready
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_major_count > constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_major_cap } }
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_two_major_snapshot
		}
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_fracture_exit_requirement = global.secret_alliance_reveal_member_count }
		divide_variable = { secret_alliance_fracture_exit_requirement = constant:secret_alliance_achievement.divide_table_denominator }
		round_variable = secret_alliance_fracture_exit_requirement
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_public_reveal_occurred
	}
}

secret_alliance_prepare_current_member_for_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_faction = yes
			is_faction_leader = no
		}
		faction_leader = { save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader_candidate }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader = event_target:secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader_candidate }
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_left_prior_faction_for_reveal
		remove_from_faction = THIS
	}
}

secret_alliance_clear_current_prior_faction_snapshot = {
	clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_left_prior_faction_for_reveal
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader
}

secret_alliance_restore_current_prior_faction = {
