	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_reveal_transaction_member
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_left_prior_faction_for_reveal
			has_variable = secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader
			var:secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader = {
				exists = yes
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
		}
		var:secret_alliance_prior_faction_leader = { add_to_faction = event_target:secret_alliance_reveal_transaction_member }
	}
	secret_alliance_clear_current_prior_faction_snapshot = yes
}

secret_alliance_create_public_faction = {
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_failed
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { secret_alliance_initialize_faction_name_grammar = yes }
	event_target:secret_alliance_leader = {
		secret_alliance_prepare_current_member_for_faction = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_rule = can_create_factions } }
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_granted_faction_creation
			set_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
		}
		create_faction_from_template = {
			template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
			name = secret_alliance_anti_target_pact_name
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_granted_faction_creation }
			clear_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
			clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_granted_faction_creation
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_leader
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_faction_leader = yes } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_failed
		}
		else = { add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_public_members = THIS } }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_leader }
			}
			secret_alliance_prepare_current_member_for_faction = yes
			event_target:secret_alliance_leader = { add_to_faction = PREV }
			if = {
				limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_leader }
				add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_public_members = THIS }
			}
			else = { set_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_failed }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_failed } }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_created
	}
}

secret_alliance_commit_public_faction_transaction = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		secret_alliance_clear_current_prior_faction_snapshot = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_rollback_failed_public_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_leader
			event_target:secret_alliance_leader = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_leader
				has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
			}
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		secret_alliance_restore_current_prior_faction = yes
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_transaction_failed }
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
	secret_alliance_cleanup_runtime_context = yes
}

secret_alliance_convert_hidden_values_to_war_state = {
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = global.secret_alliance_cohesion }
	multiply_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.resolve_cohesion_factor }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.resolve_base }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.resolve_public_commitment }
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.resolve_fracture_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_fracture_reserve > constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min } }
		subtract_from_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = global.secret_alliance_fracture_reserve }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.tentative }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.commitment_tentative_resolve }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.conditional }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.commitment_conditional_resolve }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.commitment_firm_resolve }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.irrevocable }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.commitment_irrevocable_resolve }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }

	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = global.secret_alliance_readiness }
	multiply_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.opening_readiness_factor }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_major_opening_bonus = global.secret_alliance_reveal_major_count }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_major_opening_bonus = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.opening_major_bonus }
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = secret_alliance_major_opening_bonus }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_mapped_military_surface
			event_target:secret_alliance_mapped_military_surface = { is_controlled_by = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.mapped_surface_opening_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_has_turned_channel = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.opening_turned_member_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_has_false_plan = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.opening_false_plan_penalty }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_false_plan_wartime_consequence
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_false_plan_consequence
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_turned_founder_or_major_snapshot
					has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_turned_channel_preserved_snapshot
				}
				set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_their_man_in_the_room_ready
			}
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot has_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_coordination_penalty }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }

	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses = global.secret_alliance_evidence }
	multiply_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.weakness_evidence_factor }
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_has_false_plan = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.weakness_false_plan_bonus }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_exposed_operation_surface
			event_target:secret_alliance_exposed_operation_surface = { is_controlled_by = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses = constant:secret_alliance_operation_resolution.exposed_surface_weakness_bonus }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }

	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_target_defenses = global.secret_alliance_preparedness }
	multiply_variable = { global.secret_alliance_target_defenses = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.defense_preparedness_factor }
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_continuity_sites } }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_target_defenses = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.defense_continuity_bonus }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_target_defenses min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }

	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination > constant:secret_alliance_band.readiness_mobilized } }
				add_ideas = secret_alliance_coalition_opening_coordination
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination > constant:secret_alliance_band.readiness_operational } }
				add_ideas = secret_alliance_coalition_opening_coordination_medium
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination > constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min } }
				add_ideas = secret_alliance_coalition_opening_coordination_low
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member
					}
				}
				add_ideas = secret_alliance_fractured_coalition
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		remove_ideas = secret_alliance_unexplained_interference
		remove_ideas = secret_alliance_compromised_channels
		remove_ideas = secret_alliance_hardened_networks
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_mobilized } }
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_known_enemy_plans
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } }
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_known_enemy_plans_medium
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses > constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min } }
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_known_enemy_plans_low
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_target_defenses > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_mobilized } }
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_hardened_networks
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_target_defenses > constant:secret_alliance_band.preparedness_guarded } }
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_target_defenses_medium
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_target_defenses > constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min } }
			add_ideas = secret_alliance_target_defenses_low
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_clear_protected_state_targets = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_revealed_ai = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot }
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = antagonize
				id = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.revealed_antagonize
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border }
						has_military_access_to = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					}
				}
				add_ai_strategy = { type = conquer id = event_target:secret_alliance_target value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.revealed_conquer }
				add_ai_strategy = { type = front_unit_request id = event_target:secret_alliance_target value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.revealed_front_unit_request }
				add_ai_strategy = { type = force_concentration_factor value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.revealed_role_concentration }
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_revealed_front_role_ai
			}
			else = {
				add_ai_strategy = { type = front_unit_request id = event_target:secret_alliance_target value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.revealed_support_front_unit_request }
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_revealed_support_role_ai
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_at_entry has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader }
				add_ai_strategy = { type = protect id = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader value = constant:secret_alliance_ai.revealed_sponsor_protect }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_fire_reveal_super_event = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_super_event_fired } }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_super_event_fired
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_super_event_visible_days = constant:secret_alliance_super_event.visible_days }
		set_global_flag = {
			flag = super_event_visible
			days = secret_alliance_super_event_visible_days
			value = constant:secret_alliance_super_event.public_reveal_slot
		}
		set_variable = { global.current_super_event_audio_id = constant:secret_alliance_super_event.public_reveal_audio_id }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { play_current_super_event_audio = yes }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { news_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.200 } }
	}
}

secret_alliance_join_current_member_to_existing_target_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
			NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		}
		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = event_target:secret_alliance_war_anchor
			enemy = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			single_target_only = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_join_all_members_to_existing_target_war = {
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		secret_alliance_join_current_member_to_existing_target_war = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed }
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			secret_alliance_join_current_member_to_existing_target_war = yes
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_resolve_delayed_member_call = {
	# Member scope. Hostile-war reveal never uses this path: that route calls
	# every valid member immediately and ignores delayed/turned state.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
			has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
			secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_war_anchor
			NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
		}
		secret_alliance_count_current_public_faction_survivors = yes
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_join_weight }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_withdraw_weight }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.tentative } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_tentative_join }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_tentative_withdraw }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.conditional } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_conditional_join }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_conditional_withdraw }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.firm } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_firm_join }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_firm_withdraw }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_commitment.irrevocable } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_irrevocable_join }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_irrevocable_withdraw }
		}
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_weight_max }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight min = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_weight_min max = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_weight_max }
		random_list = {
			secret_alliance_delayed_call_join_weight = {
				secret_alliance_join_current_member_to_existing_target_war = yes
				if = { limit = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target } clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call }
				else = {
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
					if = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader } remove_from_faction = THIS }
					event_target:secret_alliance_target = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 } }
				}
			}
			secret_alliance_delayed_call_withdraw_weight = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
				clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader }
					remove_from_faction = THIS
				}
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_reveal_pact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_route = secret_alliance_call_reveal_route }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute
				NOT = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.hostile_war } }
			}
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.fractured }
		}
		secret_alliance_refresh_member_validity = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count > constant:secret_alliance_state.true } }
			secret_alliance_select_reveal_leader = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_leader }
				secret_alliance_assign_preparedness_delayed_calls = yes
				secret_alliance_snapshot_reveal_state = yes
				secret_alliance_create_public_faction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_failed } }
					if = {
						limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_war_anchor }
						secret_alliance_join_all_members_to_existing_target_war = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed } }
						secret_alliance_commit_public_faction_transaction = yes
						secret_alliance_convert_hidden_values_to_war_state = yes
						set_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed
						clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_response_category_open
						clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_warning_active
						secret_alliance_apply_revealed_ai = yes
						secret_alliance_fire_reveal_super_event = yes
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_war_anchor } }
							event_target:secret_alliance_target = { activate_mission = secret_alliance_public_offensive_countdown }
						}
					}
					else = { secret_alliance_rollback_failed_public_faction = yes }
				}
				else = {
					secret_alliance_rollback_failed_public_faction = yes
				}
			}
			else = { secret_alliance_collapse_hidden_pact = yes }
		}
		else = { secret_alliance_collapse_hidden_pact = yes }
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
	}
}

secret_alliance_start_planned_coalition_war = {
	secret_alliance_count_current_public_faction_survivors = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
			NOT = { event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target } }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
		event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = {
			declare_war_on = {
				target = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				type = topple_government
			}
				save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_war_anchor
			}
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
					OR = {
						has_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_force_full_call
						AND = {
							NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member }
						}
					}
				}
				secret_alliance_join_current_member_to_existing_target_war = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member
				}
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
				clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				if = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader } remove_from_faction = THIS }
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
					add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 }
					set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_false_plan_consequence
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_delayed_call_days = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_conversion.delayed_call_days }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.50 days = secret_alliance_delayed_call_days }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member
				}
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
				clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader }
					remove_from_faction = THIS
				}
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed }
			clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.secret_alliance_members
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
						secret_alliance_is_valid_member = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member }
						NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
					}
					secret_alliance_join_current_member_to_existing_target_war = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.secret_alliance_members
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_delayed_call }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_fractured_member }
						NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
					}
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_removed
					clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
					if = { limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader } remove_from_faction = THIS }
					event_target:secret_alliance_target = { add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 } }
				}
			}
			clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_member_call_failed
		}
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.190 days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.war_pulse_days } }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# REVEALED-WAR ACTIONS, RESOLVE, SETTLEMENT, AND CLEANUP
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

secret_alliance_war_exploit_liaison_map = {
	secret_alliance_pay_war_cost = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses > constant:secret_alliance_action_value.liaison_weakness_threshold } }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.liaison_resolve_success }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.liaison_coordination_success }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.liaison_resolve_stale }
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_war_strike_known_depots = {
	secret_alliance_pay_war_cost = yes
	secret_alliance_pay_army_experience = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_forward_depot_exposed
				secret_alliance_has_false_plan = yes
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.depot_resolve_success }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.depot_coordination_success }
	}
	else = {
		add_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.depot_failure_war_support
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_war_offer_separate_terms = {
	secret_alliance_pay_diplomacy_cost = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_has_selected_suspect = yes
			var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
		}
		var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.fear }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism }
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member
						check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve < constant:secret_alliance_band.cohesion_uneasy }
					}
				}
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { white_peace = PREV }
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
					add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.separate_terms_resolve }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.separate_terms_rejected_resolve }
			}
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_war_publicize_conflicting_aims = {
	add_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.publicize_war_aims_political_power
	add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_decision_cost.diplomacy_stability_loss
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.spoils }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.publicize_war_aims_resolve }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_war_aims_public
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.publicize_war_aims_failure_resolve }
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_war_support_member_opposition = {
	secret_alliance_pay_war_cost = yes
	secret_alliance_pay_trucks = yes
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_has_selected_suspect = yes }
		var:secret_alliance_selected_suspect_scope = {
			add_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.member_opposition_war_support
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.member_opposition_resolve }
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_war_demand_sponsor_accountability = {
	secret_alliance_pay_diplomacy_cost = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major
			event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = {
				OR = {
					has_capitulated = yes
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_exposed
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.sponsor_accountability_resolve }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_accountability_crisis
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.sponsor_accountability_failure_resolve }
	}
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_war_coordinate_counteroffensive = {
	secret_alliance_pay_war_cost = yes
	add_command_power = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.counteroffensive_command_power
	add_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.counteroffensive_war_support
	add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.counteroffensive_resolve }
	secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = {
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.secret_alliance_target_defenses min = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_min max = constant:secret_alliance_band.value_max }
}

secret_alliance_run_target_ai_war_response = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_target_ai_can_respond = yes }
		secret_alliance_ai_select_action_target = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_can_start_war_action = yes
				secret_alliance_has_dynamic_army_experience_cost = yes
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_known_weaknesses > constant:secret_alliance_action_value.liaison_weakness_threshold }
			}
			secret_alliance_war_strike_known_depots = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				secret_alliance_can_start_war_diplomacy = yes
				secret_alliance_has_selected_suspect = yes
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve < constant:secret_alliance_band.cohesion_uneasy }
			}
			secret_alliance_war_offer_separate_terms = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { secret_alliance_can_start_war_action = yes }
			secret_alliance_war_exploit_liaison_map = yes
		}
		clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_ai_action_target
	}
}

secret_alliance_update_named_war_objectives = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_target_objective_progress_recorded }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { surrender_progress > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.objective_progress_threshold }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_target_objective_progress_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.target_objective_progress }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_failed_offensive_recorded }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_public_war_pulses > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.failed_offensive_pulses }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { surrender_progress < constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.failed_offensive_target_progress }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_failed_offensive_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.failed_offensive }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_war_burden_recorded }
				OR = {
					casualties > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.member_casualty_threshold
					surrender_progress > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.member_burden_progress
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_war_burden_recorded
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.member_war_burden }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_sponsors
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_aid_recorded }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_public_war_pulses > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.sponsor_aid_minimum_pulses }
				OR = {
					num_of_factories > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.sponsor_factories_threshold
					has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.sponsor_equipment_threshold }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_aid_recorded
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.sponsor_aid }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_distraction_recorded }
				any_enemy_country = { NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target } }
			}
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_distraction_recorded
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_any_sponsor_distracted
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.sponsor_distraction }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_route_survival_recorded }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_public_war_pulses > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.route_survival_minimum_pulses }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_route_survival_found = 0 }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
					has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					OR = {
						is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target
						AND = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } event_target:secret_alliance_target = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } } }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_route_survival_found = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_route_survival_found > 0 } }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_route_survival_recorded
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.route_survival }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_credible_prewar_concession
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_credible_concession_recorded }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_credible_concession_recorded
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.credible_concession }
	}
}

secret_alliance_update_two_major_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_has_two_active_majors = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_two_major_leadership_crisis }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_two_major_conflict = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_first_major_theater = event_target:secret_alliance_first_major.secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_first_major_pressure = event_target:secret_alliance_first_major.secret_alliance_sponsor_doctrine_pressure }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.secret_alliance_sponsors
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot
						NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_first_major }
						OR = {
							NOT = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_preferred_theater = secret_alliance_first_major_theater } }
							NOT = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_sponsor_doctrine_pressure = secret_alliance_first_major_pressure } }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_two_major_conflict = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_two_major_conflict > 0 }
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = secret_alliance_failed_offensive_recorded
					has_global_flag = secret_alliance_any_sponsor_distracted
					any_country = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_at_entry has_country_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_exposed }
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_two_major_leadership_crisis
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_war_aims_public
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.two_major_rivalry }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_opening_coordination = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.leadership_coordination_penalty }
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.secret_alliance_sponsors
				if = { limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot } add_ideas = secret_alliance_fractured_coalition }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_update_revealed_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { secret_alliance_is_revealed = yes }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_public_war_pulses = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			secret_alliance_apply_coalition_victory_outcome = yes
		}
		else = {
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { secret_alliance_run_target_ai_war_response = yes }
			secret_alliance_update_named_war_objectives = yes
			secret_alliance_update_two_major_crisis = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = secret_alliance_first_major
					NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_collapsing }
					event_target:secret_alliance_first_major = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.sponsor_capital_loss }
				set_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_collapsing
			}
			for_each_scope_loop = {
				array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_capital_loss_recorded }
						surrender_progress > constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.member_failure_threshold
					}
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_member_capital_loss_recorded
					add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.member_capital_loss }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_war_aims_public
					NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_promises_resolved }
				}
				set_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_promises_resolved
				add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.conflicting_promises }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_public_war_pulses > constant:secret_alliance_achievement.opening_survival_pulses } }
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_opening_survived }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve < constant:secret_alliance_band.resolve_fracture } }
				secret_alliance_try_wartime_fracture_exit = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve < constant:secret_alliance_band.resolve_collapse } }
				secret_alliance_apply_internal_rupture_outcome = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { secret_alliance_target_war_is_over = yes }
				secret_alliance_apply_target_victory_outcome = yes
			}
			else = {
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.190 days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.war_pulse_days } }
			}
		}
		secret_alliance_clamp_war_values = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_try_wartime_fracture_exit = {
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_fracture_exit_pool
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.fear }
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.grievance }
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism }
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_turned_member
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_fracture_exit_pool = THIS }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_fracture_exit_pool^num > 0 } }
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = secret_alliance_fracture_exit_pool
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { white_peace = PREV }
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.fracture_exit_resolve }
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_fracture_exit_pool
}

secret_alliance_record_starting_capital_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:secret_alliance_target_starting_capital = {
				is_controlled_by = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_kept_starting_capital
	}
}

secret_alliance_count_current_public_faction_survivors = {
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count = constant:secret_alliance_state.false }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
			}
			faction_leader = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
					OR = {
						tag = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
						is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count = constant:secret_alliance_state.true }
			}
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_apply_target_victory_outcome = {
	secret_alliance_count_current_public_faction_survivors = yes
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_settled
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_victory
		secret_alliance_record_starting_capital_outcome = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_two_major_snapshot
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_normal_origin
					has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_high_or_maximum
				}
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_kept_starting_capital
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve < constant:secret_alliance_band.resolve_collapse }
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_two_giants_one_grave_ready
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.minimum_active_members }
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_internal_rupture }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve < constant:secret_alliance_band.resolve_collapse }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.dissolved }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_dissolved
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.191 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_collapsing
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_accountability_crisis
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.leadership_split }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_leadership_split_settlement
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.195 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count > 0 }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.negotiated_settlement }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_negotiated_settlement
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.193 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve
				value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_resolve
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count
				value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_members
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment }
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
			event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = {
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.continued_regional_bloc }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_negotiated_settlement
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_continued_regional_bloc
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.196 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.dissolved }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_dissolved
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.191 }
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_record_surrounded_resolution_achievement = yes
	secret_alliance_finalize_outcome = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_coalition_victory_outcome = {
	secret_alliance_count_current_public_faction_survivors = yes
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_settled
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_victory }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.minimum_active_members } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.dissolved }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_dissolved
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.197 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_collapsing
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_sponsor_accountability_crisis
				has_global_flag = secret_alliance_two_major_leadership_crisis
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.leadership_split }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_leadership_split_settlement
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.198 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.spoils } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count
					value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_members
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve
					value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_resolve
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_war_aims_public }
			}
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.coalition_victory_bloc }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.192 } }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.leadership_split }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_leadership_split_settlement
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.198 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve
					value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_resolve
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count
					value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_members
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
				event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = { is_faction_leader = yes NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.continued_regional_bloc }
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_security_goal_completed
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_negotiated_settlement
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_continued_regional_bloc
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.196 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.dissolved }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_dissolved
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.197 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.regime_pressure } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count
					value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_members
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
				event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = {
					is_faction_leader = yes
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_major_snapshot
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve
					value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_resolve
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.coalition_victory_bloc }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.192 } }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.leadership_split }
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_leadership_split_settlement
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.198 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_survivor_count
				value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_members
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
			event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = { is_faction_leader = yes NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			check_variable = {
				var = global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve
				value = constant:secret_alliance_resolve_fact.continued_bloc_minimum_resolve
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.coalition_victory_bloc }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.192 } }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.dissolved }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_dissolved
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.197 }
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_finalize_outcome = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_negotiated_settlement_outcome = {
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_settled
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.negotiated_settlement }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_negotiated_settlement
		secret_alliance_record_starting_capital_outcome = yes
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.193 }
	}
	secret_alliance_finalize_outcome = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_internal_rupture_outcome = {
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_coalition_resolve = 0 }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				has_war_with = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			}
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { white_peace = PREV }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit }
				}
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit
				event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
					add_to_variable = { secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_internal_rupture }
	secret_alliance_apply_target_victory_outcome = yes
}

secret_alliance_check_divide_table_achievement = {
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = secret_alliance_event_fracture_exit_count
					value = secret_alliance_fracture_exit_requirement
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_victory }
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_divide_table_ready
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_record_surrounded_resolution_achievement = {
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_maximum_composition_qualified
				has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_opening_survived
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_kept_starting_capital
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_human_consent_bypassed }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.dissolved }
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.negotiated_settlement }
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.continued_regional_bloc }
					check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.leadership_split }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_resolution_qualified
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_surrounded_not_buried_ready
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_promote_public_faction_to_postwar_bloc = {
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_active
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_postwar_members
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_postwar_member_count = 0 }
	event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = {
		save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_postwar_leader
		set_faction_name = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_name
		set_faction_manifest = secret_alliance_postwar_security_manifest
		remove_faction_goal = secret_alliance_goal_containment
		remove_faction_goal = secret_alliance_goal_punitive
		remove_faction_goal = secret_alliance_goal_regime_pressure
		remove_faction_goal = secret_alliance_goal_spoils
		add_faction_goal = secret_alliance_goal_postwar_security
		set_faction_rule = secret_alliance_postwar_joining_rule
		set_faction_rule = secret_alliance_postwar_war_declaration_rule
		set_faction_rule = secret_alliance_postwar_call_to_war_rule
		set_faction_rule = secret_alliance_postwar_change_leader_rule
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_public_members
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
				OR = {
					tag = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
					is_in_faction_with = event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_member
			add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_postwar_members = THIS }
			add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_postwar_member_count = 1 }
		}
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = {
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_member
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_leader
	}
	secret_alliance_refresh_postwar_bloc_state = yes
}

secret_alliance_clear_postwar_bloc_state = {
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_cleanup_in_progress
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_postwar_members
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_member
		clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_leader
	}
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_postwar_members
	clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_postwar_member_count
	clear_global_event_target = secret_alliance_postwar_leader
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_active
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_security_goal_completed
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_punitive_goal_completed
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_regime_goal_completed
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_spoils_goal_completed
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_cleanup_in_progress
}

secret_alliance_refresh_postwar_bloc_state = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_cleanup_in_progress }
		}
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_postwar_survivors
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_postwar_survivor_count = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_postwar_leader_found = 0 }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_postwar_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
					has_faction_goal = secret_alliance_goal_postwar_security
				}
				set_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_member
				add_to_temp_array = { secret_alliance_postwar_survivors = THIS }
				add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_postwar_survivor_count = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_postwar_leader_found = 1 }
					set_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_leader
					save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_postwar_leader
				}
				else = { clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_leader }
			}
			else = {
				clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_member
				clr_country_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_leader
			}
		}
		clear_array = global.secret_alliance_postwar_members
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = secret_alliance_postwar_survivors
			add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_postwar_members = THIS }
		}
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_postwar_member_count = secret_alliance_postwar_survivor_count }
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_postwar_survivors
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_postwar_survivor_count < constant:secret_alliance_event.minimum_active_members }
					check_variable = { secret_alliance_postwar_leader_found < constant:secret_alliance_state.true }
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_cleanup_in_progress
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = secret_alliance_postwar_leader
					event_target:secret_alliance_postwar_leader = {
						is_faction_leader = yes
						has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
					}
				}
				event_target:secret_alliance_postwar_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
			}
			secret_alliance_clear_postwar_bloc_state = yes
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_finalize_outcome = {
	# Faction dismantling and postwar conversion fire faction lifecycle on-actions
	# synchronously. Reuse the transaction guard until runtime cleanup finishes.
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress
	secret_alliance_count_current_public_faction_survivors = yes
	secret_alliance_check_divide_table_achievement = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.coalition_victory_bloc }
				check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.continued_regional_bloc }
			}
		}
		secret_alliance_promote_public_faction_to_postwar_bloc = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_created
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader
			event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_secret_alliance_anti_target_pact
			}
			NOT = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.coalition_victory_bloc } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_final_outcome = constant:secret_alliance_final_outcome.continued_regional_bloc } }
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_current_public_faction_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_public_faction_dissolved
	}
	secret_alliance_cleanup_runtime_context = yes
}

secret_alliance_prepare_hidden_collapse_aftermath = {
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_aftermath_identified_participants
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_aftermath_innocent_suspects
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_evidence_snapshot = global.secret_alliance_evidence }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_preparedness_snapshot = global.secret_alliance_preparedness }
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_confirmed_members
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes }
			add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_aftermath_identified_participants = THIS }
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.secret_alliance_members value = THIS } }
			}
			add_to_array = { global.secret_alliance_aftermath_innocent_suspects = THIS }
		}
	}
}

secret_alliance_finish_hidden_collapse_aftermath = {
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_aftermath_identified_participants
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_aftermath_innocent_suspects
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_aftermath_evidence_snapshot
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_aftermath_preparedness_snapshot
}

secret_alliance_aftermath_expose_evidence = {
	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_aftermath_target
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_aftermath_evidence_exposed
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_political_power = secret_alliance_aftermath_evidence_snapshot }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.expose_political_power_per_evidence }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.expose_political_power_base }
	add_political_power = secret_alliance_aftermath_political_power
	add_war_support = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.expose_war_support
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_aftermath_identified_participants
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_aftermath_target } }
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_aftermath_target modifier = large_decrease }
			event_target:secret_alliance_aftermath_target = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = PREV modifier = large_decrease } }
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_finish_hidden_collapse_aftermath = yes
}

secret_alliance_aftermath_keep_secret = {
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_aftermath_kept_secret
	add_political_power = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.secrecy_political_power
	add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.secrecy_stability
	secret_alliance_finish_hidden_collapse_aftermath = yes
}

secret_alliance_aftermath_repair_innocent_relations = {
	save_event_target_as = secret_alliance_aftermath_target
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_aftermath_repaired_innocent_relations
	add_stability = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.repair_stability
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_aftermath_innocent_suspects
		if = {
			limit = { exists = yes NOT = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_aftermath_target } }
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = event_target:secret_alliance_aftermath_target modifier = medium_increase }
			event_target:secret_alliance_aftermath_target = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = PREV modifier = medium_increase } }
		}
	}
	secret_alliance_finish_hidden_collapse_aftermath = yes
}

secret_alliance_aftermath_retain_counterintelligence = {
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_retained
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_days = secret_alliance_aftermath_preparedness_snapshot }
	multiply_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_days = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.counterintelligence_days_per_preparedness }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_days = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.counterintelligence_base_days }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_days
		min = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.counterintelligence_base_days
		max = constant:secret_alliance_aftermath.counterintelligence_max_days
	}
	round_temp_variable = secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_days
	add_timed_idea = {
		idea = secret_alliance_retained_counterintelligence
		days = secret_alliance_aftermath_counterintelligence_days
	}
	secret_alliance_finish_hidden_collapse_aftermath = yes
}

secret_alliance_collapse_hidden_pact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed }
		}
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_collapsed
		secret_alliance_prepare_hidden_collapse_aftermath = yes
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_hidden_pact_collapsed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_normal_origin
					has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_true_founder_confirmed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_innocent_named }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_innocent_attacked }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_unconfirmed_suspect_annexed }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_forced_origin }
				}
				set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_empty_chair_ready
			}
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.194 }
		}
		secret_alliance_cleanup_runtime_context = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_remove_current_member_runtime = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_event_granted_faction_creation }
		clear_rule = { can_create_factions = yes }
	}

secret_alliance_cancel_target_missions = {
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = secret_alliance_watch_liaison_route } remove_mission = secret_alliance_watch_liaison_route }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = secret_alliance_seize_compromised_courier } remove_mission = secret_alliance_seize_compromised_courier }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = secret_alliance_turn_recruited_clerk } remove_mission = secret_alliance_turn_recruited_clerk }
	if = { limit = { has_active_mission = secret_alliance_protect_defecting_envoy } remove_mission = secret_alliance_protect_defecting_envoy }

		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_settled
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_collapsed
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_coalition_warning_active
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_force_full_call
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_launch_in_progress
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_regional_ring

		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_member_count

		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_preparedness
		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_war_pressure
		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_evolution_stage
