		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_dispute_commitment_a
		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_dispute_commitment_b
		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_last_invitation_outcome
		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_last_sponsor_outcome
		clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_fracture_reserve
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_active } }
			clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_security_goal_completed
			clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_punitive_goal_completed
			clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_regime_goal_completed
			clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_spoils_goal_completed
		}
		clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_cleanup_in_progress
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TRIGGERABLE SCENARIO: COALITION UNMASKED
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

secret_alliance_prepare_scenario_counts = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.medium_members }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.medium_majors }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.low } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.low_members }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.low_majors }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.high } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.high_members }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.high_majors }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.maximum_members }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.maximum_majors }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.great_power_sponsor } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors < constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_major_cap } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_ii_major_cap }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot = secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors_snapshot = secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors }
}

secret_alliance_count_scenario_safe_pool = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_minor_pool = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_major_pool = 0 }
	every_country = {
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_minor_recruit = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_minor_pool = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { secret_alliance_is_valid_major_sponsor = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_major_pool = 1 } }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_selected_majors = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_major_pool }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_selected_majors min = 0 max = secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors_snapshot }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_minor_pool }
	add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_selected_majors }
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot = ROOT.secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors_snapshot = ROOT.secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors_snapshot }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool_snapshot = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_major_pool_snapshot = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_major_pool }
	}
}

secret_alliance_select_scenario_composition = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_selected_majors = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_major_selection_complete = 0 }
	secret_alliance_build_weighted_major_member_pool = yes
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_selected_majors < secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors_snapshot } check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_major_selection_complete < 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_previous_count = global.secret_alliance_major_count }
		secret_alliance_draw_weighted_major_member_from_pool = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count > secret_alliance_scenario_previous_count } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_selected_majors = 1 }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_major_selection_complete = 1 } }
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_major_batch_pool
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.unlikely_coalition }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot }
		}
		secret_alliance_build_weighted_matching_ideology_minor_pool = yes
		secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = yes
		clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot } }
			secret_alliance_build_weighted_opposing_ideology_minor_pool = yes
			secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = yes
			clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_member_selection_complete = 0 }
	secret_alliance_build_weighted_minor_member_pool = yes
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count < secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot } check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_member_selection_complete < 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_previous_count = global.secret_alliance_member_count }
		secret_alliance_draw_weighted_minor_member_from_pool = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = secret_alliance_scenario_previous_count } }
			set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_member_selection_complete = 1 }
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = secret_alliance_weighted_minor_batch_pool
}

secret_alliance_apply_scenario_starting_values = {
	clear_variable = secret_alliance_preparedness_baseline
	clear_variable = global.secret_alliance_fracture_reserve
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_medium_resolve }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_medium_readiness }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.low } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_low_resolve }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_low_readiness }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.high } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_high_resolve }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_high_readiness }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_maximum_resolve }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_readiness = constant:secret_alliance_starting_values.scenario_maximum_readiness }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.unlikely_coalition } }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_action_value.scenario_unlikely_cohesion }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute
	}
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_alertness = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evidence = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_preparedness = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_war_pressure = constant:secret_alliance_band.war_pressure_war }
	secret_alliance_clamp_core_values = yes
}

secret_alliance_apply_scenario_identity = {
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_support_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.medium_support_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_train_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.medium_trains }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_truck_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.medium_trucks }
	set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_fuel_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.medium_fuel }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.low } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_support_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.low_support_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_train_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.low_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_truck_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.low_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_fuel_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.low_fuel }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.high } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_support_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.high_support_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_train_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.high_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_truck_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.high_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_fuel_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.high_fuel }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_support_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.maximum_support_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_train_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.maximum_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_truck_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.maximum_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_fuel_package = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_scale.maximum_fuel }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.regional_ring } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.containment }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_regional_ring
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor } }
				if = {
					limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border }
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.land_access }
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics }
					if = {
						limit = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
						set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.maritime_access }
					}
					else = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.transport_access } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.ideological_front } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.regime_pressure }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_ideological_front
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor } }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.great_power_sponsor } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.punitive }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_great_power_sponsor
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor } }
				if = {
					limit = { is_neighbor_of = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.border }
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.land_access }
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics }
					if = {
						limit = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
						set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.maritime_access }
					}
					else = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.transport_access } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_type.unlikely_coalition } }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_doctrine = constant:secret_alliance_doctrine.spoils }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_unlikely_coalition
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_recent_dispute
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_conflicting_war_aims_public
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_fracture_reserve = constant:secret_alliance_scenario_identity.unlikely_fracture_reserve }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_major_sponsor }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.patronage }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.sponsor_protection }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_same_ideology = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.opportunism }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.postwar_gain }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.logistics }
				if = {
					limit = { any_owned_state = { is_coastal = yes } }
					set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.maritime_access }
				}
				else = { set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.transport_access } }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_motive = constant:secret_alliance_motive.ideology }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_private_commitment = constant:secret_alliance_private_commitment.political_change }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_role = constant:secret_alliance_role.political }
				set_variable = { secret_alliance_support_capability = constant:secret_alliance_support_capability.political_access }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_random_coalition
		secret_alliance_determine_doctrine = yes
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.secret_alliance_members
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = secret_alliance_scenario_support_package }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = secret_alliance_scenario_train_package }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = secret_alliance_scenario_truck_package }
		add_fuel = secret_alliance_scenario_fuel_package
	}
	event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_achieved_members = global.secret_alliance_member_count }
		set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_achieved_majors = global.secret_alliance_major_count }
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_ai_only_composition
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_maximum
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_achieved_majors = secret_alliance_scenario_requested_majors_snapshot }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = secret_alliance_scenario_achieved_members value = secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool_snapshot < secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot }
						check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_achieved_members = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool_snapshot }
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_maximum_composition_qualified
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_maximum
				check_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_safe_total_pool_snapshot < secret_alliance_scenario_requested_members_snapshot }
			}
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_safe_pool_limited
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr11.201 days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.scenario_composition_notice_days }
		}
	}
}

trigger_secret_alliance_coalition_unmasked_scenario = {
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_launch_in_progress
	save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_target
	secret_alliance_reset_target_run_snapshots = yes
	set_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_country
	secret_alliance_initialize_faction_name_grammar = yes
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_members
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_member_cleanup_archive
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspects
	clear_array = global.secret_alliance_suspect_cleanup_archive
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count = 0 }
	set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = 0 }
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
	set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_origin
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_normal_origin
	clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_normal_origin
	clr_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_forced_origin
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_type_snapshot = triggerable_scenarios_secret_alliance_type }
	set_variable = { secret_alliance_scenario_intensity_snapshot = triggerable_scenarios_intensity }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
		set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_maximum
		set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_high_or_maximum
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.high } }
		set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_scenario_high_or_maximum
	}
	secret_alliance_prepare_scenario_counts = yes
	secret_alliance_count_scenario_safe_pool = yes
	secret_alliance_select_scenario_composition = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { triggerable_scenarios_intensity = constant:triggerable_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.secret_alliance_members
			set_country_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_maximum_member
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_member_count > constant:secret_alliance_event.minimum_active_members }
			check_variable = { global.secret_alliance_major_count = secret_alliance_scenario_selected_majors }
		}
		secret_alliance_apply_scenario_starting_values = yes
		secret_alliance_apply_scenario_identity = yes
		capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_target_starting_capital }
		set_variable = { global.secret_alliance_evolution_stage = constant:secret_alliance_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_i
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_ii
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_evolution_iii
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_response_category_open
		set_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_force_full_call
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.public_conference }
		secret_alliance_reveal_pact = yes
		secret_alliance_start_planned_coalition_war = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_composition_failed
		secret_alliance_abort_unstarted_context = yes
	}
	clr_global_flag = secret_alliance_scenario_launch_in_progress
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# NARROW ON-ACTION HANDLERS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

secret_alliance_handle_war_relation_added = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_target_aggression_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.target_aggression_days }
		set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recent_target_aggression days = secret_alliance_target_aggression_days }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.target_aggression_cohesion }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			OR = {
				AND = {
					tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					FROM = {
						has_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_lead_source
						NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_false_lead_source
					NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
					FROM = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_innocent_attacked }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
					FROM = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
				}
				AND = {
					tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target
					FROM = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
			save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_war_anchor
		}
		else = { FROM = { save_global_event_target_as = secret_alliance_war_anchor } }
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_call_reveal_route = constant:secret_alliance_reveal_route.hostile_war }
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { secret_alliance_reveal_pact = yes }
	}
}

secret_alliance_handle_annex = {
	# on_annex uses ROOT as the annexer and FROM as the annexed country. Keep
	# this achievement disqualifier out of the shared faction/state callback.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_target
			tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target
			FROM = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_suspect
				NOT = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_confirmed_by_target }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = achievement_secret_alliance_unconfirmed_suspect_annexed
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			secret_alliance_is_concealed = yes
			tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target
		}
		set_temp_variable = { secret_alliance_target_aggression_days = constant:secret_alliance_operation.target_aggression_days }
		set_global_flag = { flag = secret_alliance_recent_target_aggression days = secret_alliance_target_aggression_days }
		add_to_variable = { global.secret_alliance_cohesion = constant:secret_alliance_member_change.target_aggression_cohesion }
	}
	secret_alliance_handle_country_state_change = yes
}

secret_alliance_handle_country_state_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress }
		}
		secret_alliance_refresh_suspect_validity = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress }
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_target
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = {
				OR = {
					has_capitulated = yes
					exists = no
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed }
			secret_alliance_apply_coalition_victory_outcome = yes
		}
		else = {
			event_target:secret_alliance_target = { set_country_flag = secret_alliance_target_invalidated }
			secret_alliance_cleanup_runtime_context = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_in_progress }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member
				has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot
				tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target
				FROM = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_active_member }
				FROM = { has_country_flag = secret_alliance_reveal_member_snapshot }
				FROM = { tag = event_target:secret_alliance_target }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed }
			secret_alliance_update_revealed_war = yes
		}
		else = { secret_alliance_refresh_member_validity = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_active }
		secret_alliance_refresh_postwar_bloc_state = yes
	}
}

secret_alliance_handle_peaceconference_ended = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = secret_alliance_revealed
			has_event_target = secret_alliance_target
		}
		event_target:secret_alliance_target = { secret_alliance_update_revealed_war = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = secret_alliance_postwar_bloc_active }
		secret_alliance_refresh_postwar_bloc_state = yes
	}
}
